- **WS-R33, Suite billing end to end (2026-09-04)** — WS-R28 built the Suite (`vy_org`, members, the attach predicate) and `vy_org_subscription`'s own table and read, deliberately with no writer and no caller for `seatCoversCreatorTier`; this workstream builds the money. Migration 095: `vy_creator_subscription` (owner lane — `owner_user_id`+`replica_id`, no person column, deleted BY NAME in `api/_replica-full-erasure.js` on `vy_room_price`'s own precedent, never in `PERSON_TABLES`, `ws-r33-creator-subscription-owner-lane`), plan in `('room','studio')` at ₹4,999/₹19,999 (`'institute'` has no self-serve price, sold to a Suite only); `vy_payment_event` widened rather than duplicated — `room_id`/`subscription_id` (follower lane) made nullable, `org_id` (nullable, `on delete set null`, exactly the brief's own words) and a new `org_subscription_id` (nullable, real FK cascade) added for the Suite lane, a CHECK (`vy_payment_event_one_lane`) making the two lanes mutually exclusive rather than dropping `subscription_id`'s existing FK to widen it across two tables — a design tried and rejected first (`ws-r33-widening-subscription-id-fk-instead-of-a-new-column`) because three prior workstreams' own CTEs depend on that cascade. `api/_payments.js` gains `startOrgSubscription`/`updateOrgSeats` (a Suite's own seat subscription, idempotent on the org, seat reductions refused below the count already in use, seat additions sent to the provider FIRST and the local row only ever updated with what it accepted) and `startCreatorSubscription` (refuses BEFORE any provider call when `seatCoversCreatorTier` — WS-R28's own predicate, unwired until now — reports covered; `'institute'` refused, no fixed price). The provider seam (`api/_payments/providers/{fake,razorpay}.js`) widened its `createSubscription` input from `{priceInr, roomSlug, followerId}` to `{priceInr, label, ref}` so all three lanes share one interface, never a second provider client (`ws-r33-provider-seam-generalized-to-label-ref`); `razorpay.js` also gains `updateSubscriptionQuantity` (`PATCH /v1/subscriptions/:id`), explicitly marked NOT VERIFIED — no fresh doc fetch was performed for it this session, unlike every dated endpoint above it. `applyWebhook` now resolves one of three lanes by `(provider, provider_subscription_ref)` (follower, then Suite, then creator tier) before applying: the follower lane is byte-identical to WS-R11/WS-R30's own code; the Suite lane writes a ledger row with `platform_take_inr = amount_inr`, `creator_share_inr = 0` — v0 does not distribute a Suite's seat revenue across its attached creators, a scope cut named rather than an invented formula (`ws-r33-suite-seat-revenue-not-distributed-to-creators`); the creator tier lane is a plain state UPDATE with NO ledger row at all, since a creator's own subscription has no second party to split revenue with (`ws-r33-creator-tier-charge-has-no-ledger-row`). `api/_org.js`'s `attachRoom`/`orgBoard`/`listMyOrgs` now read a coalesced seat cap (`seatCapSql`, one fragment, substituted everywhere the cap is read so the write and every read can never disagree): an ACTIVE subscription's own seats raises or lowers the cap; a subscription that never authenticated does NOT raise it (falls through to `seat_limit`, the brief's own required negative control); a subscription that lapsed drops the cap to ZERO without detaching a single already-attached Room (`ws-r33-coalesced-seat-cap-three-way-not-boolean`). `api/_creator-tier.js` (new): `creatorTierFromRows` (pure: covered/free/plan-name) plus `readCreatorTier` (the thin DB wrapper). HTTP doors: `api/org.js` gains `start_subscription`/`update_seats`; `api/payments.js`'s GET gains `creator_tier` alongside `price`/`revenue`, and a `start_creator_subscription` op. Studio: `SuiteCard.tsx` gains a "Show money" section per Suite (seats used/seats paid, next charge date+amount, the 25% take line, start-subscription and add-seat controls) and its seats line now reads the coalesced `seats_paid` rather than the static `seat_limit`; `RoomStudio.tsx`'s address card gains the tier sentence ("Your seat in `<Suite>` covers this Room" when exempt; otherwise the real tier or two upgrade buttons at the two real prices). `evals/org-billing/run.mjs` (new, registered): 40/40 offline — the seam twins, the coalesced cap at the exact boundary (paid 3, the 4th refused), the lapse behaviour (Rooms stay, attach stops), the exemption (NEGATIVE CONTROL c: refused before any provider call, the only db call made is the exemption's own read), the three-lane webhook resolution, the take arithmetic, and NEGATIVE CONTROLS (a) an unsigned webhook writes nothing to any billing table and (b) a `created` Suite subscription never raises the cap. Two existing suites needed small, non-behavioural fixture updates to stay green: `evals/payments/run.mjs`'s fake `db` gained two lines so an unknown ref still falls cleanly through all three new lookups (still 62/62, follower-lane SQL byte-identical to before); `evals/org/run.mjs`'s fake `db` gained an `effectiveSeatCap` helper mirroring `seatCapSql` exactly so its own fixtures (none of which seed `orgSubscriptions`) keep behaving as before (still 54/54). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **16/16 on the untouched tree and 16/16 after** every file in this report (`measurements.md#ws-r33-org-billing-offline-eval-2026-09-04`). `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls, unchanged. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1007 nodes / 1237 edges. `node evals/sqlcast.mjs`: 163 tables (up from 162 on the untouched tree, measured by reconstructing HEAD's `db/schema.sql`+migrations in an isolated temp dir), 0 conflicts, 0 uncast sites. `node evals/persontables.mjs`: 132 person-keyed tables (73 owner lane, up from 72), 56 manifest entries (unchanged — the new table is deliberately NOT in `PERSON_TABLES`). `node evals/room-leak/run.mjs`: 78/78 unchanged (neither new file names `vy_room_follower`/`vy_room_thread`). Deliberately unbuilt and logged, not silently missing: `renewedUnasked` (`api/_phase-gate.js`, WS-R30) was NOT wired to read this workstream's new `vy_creator_subscription` table — that integration is a real remaining gap the brief did not ask this workstream to close, and the honest zero WS-R30 shipped stays a zero until a future session closes it; Suite seat revenue distribution to attached creators (named above); a per-lane dispute/reconciliation history for creator-tier charges. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: no statement in migration 095 or any function this workstream added has ever run against a live Postgres (no `NEON_URL` in this environment; every new SQL statement is listed verbatim in this workstream's final report for the main loop to `EXPLAIN`); no real `vy_creator_subscription` row and no real Suite-lane `vy_payment_event` row exist anywhere outside a fake `db`; no human has ever seen `SuiteCard.tsx`'s new money section or `RoomStudio.tsx`'s new tier sentence render in a browser; `scripts/relcheck.mjs` did not run (no `NEON_URL`); Razorpay's `PATCH /v1/subscriptions/:id` quantity-update endpoint was never fetched from the provider's own docs this session. Did not push; on branch `ws-r33-suite-billing`. No new env vars (the existing `PAYMENTS_*` names carry).

**2026-09-04, WS-R33 merged (Suite billing, and the creator tier's one caller).** Third of wave eight, merged over the WS-R34 tip. Conflicts only in the context files (unioned from the wave-seven base, 0 duplicated headings), the graph (+9 nodes, +9 edges) and the suite registry (both kept); the schema block and the erasure job merged on their own. Migration 095 adds `vy_creator_subscription` (owner lane, no person column) and widens the payment ledger into two mutually exclusive lanes under one CHECK; the provider seam is generalised to a label and a ref so the same two twins serve followers, Suites and creators; the seat cap is a three-way coalesce inside the attach predicate (an active subscription's seats, zero when lapsed, the static limit when nothing was ever authenticated), so a Suite whose card expired keeps every published Room and admits no new one; the creator tier charge refuses before any provider call when a seat covers the creator, which is the one caller the WS-R28 predicate was built for. Applied live in one transaction of 21 statements and read back; fourteen statements EXPLAINed, the coalesce visible in the attach plan. Gate 16/16 on the merged tree; org-billing 40/40, org 54/54, payments 62/62, replica erasure 20/20, room-leak 78/78, phase-gate 49/49, persontables 56 entries, sqlcast 0 uncast. Deliberately unbuilt and logged: Suite seat revenue is not distributed to creators' payouts (no formula exists yet), creator-tier charges write no ledger row, and WS-R30's renewed-unasked stays an honest zero rather than reading the new table. R35 (097) is last.

- **WS-R35, Pulse v1: k-anonymous label combinations (migration 097, 2026-09-04)** — v0's floor (n>=5 per single topic, WS-R17/migration 080) does not stop two individually-safe buckets read TOGETHER from naming someone: "5 asked about visas" and "5 asked about divorce" can share one person. Migration 097 tightens v0's `vy_room_pulse_topic` (label length 1-60 to 2-32, and the count cap from an app-only 8 to a STRUCTURAL 12 via a new `slot smallint` column CHECKed 1..12 plus a unique index on `(room_id, slot)` — both new CHECKs added `not valid` since this session cannot confirm no live row would violate a tighter bound than v0 shipped with, `decisions.md#ws-r35-label-bounds-added-not-valid`) and adds two new content-free tables: `vy_room_pulse_week` (one header row per Room per week, carrying only a `suppressed` count) and `vy_room_pulse_combo` (a count over a SORTED `text[]` of 1-3 label TEXTS captured at publish time, never a `topic_id` FK, so a later rename can never rewrite an already-published week). `api/_pulse.js` gains the k-anonymous publish: `publishCombo` is ONE `insert ... select ... having` statement per candidate — the row is inserted or it is not, decided entirely inside that statement's own `having` clause (own population >=5 AND, for every OTHER active label, adding it never produces a population of 1-4), never by a JS read-then-branch; every literal column is `min(...)`-wrapped and the one real aggregate is `count(*)`, the same technique WS-R25's `min(joined_at)` precedent uses one column over, applied here to a whole row (`decisions.md#ws-r35-pairwise-check-is-set-vs-single-label` names the deliberately narrower scope: checked against every OTHER SINGLE label, not every other multi-label candidate too). `computeComboSnapshot` generates size-1 and size-2 candidates only, even though the schema allows up to 3 (`decisions.md#ws-r35-combo-size-capped-at-two`, a Phase-0-scale round-trip-budget call); `runPulseSweep` now calls it ALONGSIDE v0's unchanged `computeSnapshot`, never replacing it (`decisions.md#ws-r35-v0-snapshot-kept-not-replaced`). `weeklyNote(rows, opts)` is a pure function (no database access, computed fresh at read time rather than stored, `decisions.md#ws-r35-note-computed-at-read-not-stored`) producing one English paragraph from a closed 3-action list (a check-in design, the interview, a never-rule) over this week's published counts only. Law 5 (withdrawing opt-in narrows only future publishes) needed zero new code — v0's `setOptIn`/`revoke` are untouched and every v1 read already filters `revoked_at is null` (`decisions.md#ws-r35-withdraw-is-free-via-v0-revoke`). One real defect caught and fixed before the gate ever ran: a first draft factored the shared "person matches every label" SQL fragment into a helper function, which broke `evals/room-leak/run.mjs`'s static text-scan parser two ways at once — the helper's own tiny literal failed on its own non-aggregate select, and the CALLER's literal no longer contained the watched table name in source text and silently escaped the scan entirely (`rejected.md#ws-r35-pulse-combo-sql-factored-through-a-helper-evaded-the-leak-batterys-static-scan`); fixed by writing the fragment out inline, longhand, in all three call sites, and caught by hand-running the parser's own regex against the file BEFORE the real battery did — kept as `evals/pulse/run.mjs`'s new negative control (vii). A second, smaller defect of the SAME recurring shape this repo has now hit four times (`rejected.md#ws-r1-backtick-inside-a-sql-comment-inside-a-js-template-literal`, `#ws-r2-sql-comment-backticks-terminate-the-template-literal`, and WS-R28's own session log): a markdown-style backtick pair inside a `--` SQL comment inside `api/_replica-full-erasure.js`'s giant JS template literal terminated the string early, caught by `node --check` before any eval ran. `evals/pulse/run.mjs`: 51/51 (19 pre-existing v0 assertions unchanged, 32 new — the intersection boundary at overlap 0/1/5, label bounds, rename-keeps-history, withdraw-narrows-future-only, the note's closed action list, and two negative controls, `measurements.md#ws-r35-pulse-offline-eval-2026-09-04`). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **16/16 on the untouched tree and 16/16 after** every file in this report, both without `NEON_URL` (`measurements.md#ws-r35-gate-2026-09-04`). `node evals/room-leak/run.mjs`: 78/78 unchanged. `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls, unchanged. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1008 nodes / 1237 edges. Front end: `src/studio/pulseApi.ts` gains `PulseComboBucket`/mirrored `PULSE_MAX_LABELS`/`PULSE_LABEL_MAX_LEN` constants (the front end cannot import a server module, so these two numbers are mirrored rather than shared — a drift risk logged, not solved); `RoomStudio.tsx`'s Pulse card now shows combo sets (a 2-label bucket renders as "label and label"), the suppressed count as a sentence, and the weekly note, and its "add a topic" cutoffs move from the old 8/60 to 12/32. No new env vars. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: no statement in migration 097 has ever run against a live Postgres (no `NEON_URL` in this environment; every new SQL statement is listed verbatim in this workstream's final report for the main loop to `EXPLAIN`); no real `vy_room_pulse_week`/`vy_room_pulse_combo` row exists outside a fake `db`; no real creator has ever seen the combo card, the suppressed sentence, or the weekly note in a browser; `scripts/relcheck.mjs` did not run (no `NEON_URL`). Did not push; on branch `ws-r35-pulse-v1`.

**2026-09-04, WS-R35 merged (Pulse v1, k-anonymous label combinations).** Last of wave eight, merged over the WS-R33 tip. Conflicts only in the context files (unioned from the wave-seven base, 0 duplicated headings), the graph (+10 nodes, +9 edges) and the schema (094, 096, then 097, all kept); the erasure job's two new CTEs merged on their own beside R33's. A count is now over a SET of label texts captured at publish time, written only when its own population is at least five AND no other active label would carve a population of one to four out of it, both decided inside the INSERT's own HAVING; refused rows are counted in a `suppressed` integer on the week header; twelve labels per Room as a slot column with a unique index; the weekly note is pure over published counts with a closed action list; withdrawing opt-in narrows future publishes and leaves past counts. The live EXPLAIN at the merge refused the publish statement as written (`min(uuid)` does not exist in Postgres; the fake db could not know), fixed as `min(($n)::text)::uuid` and re-planned; logged as a rejection with the rule. The live topic table was empty, so both `not valid` CHECKs were validated at the merge and the two statements added to the migration. Gate 16/16 on the merged tree; pulse 51/51 (was 19), room-leak 78/78, replica erasure 20/20, org-billing 40/40, room-export ok, sqlcast 0 uncast. Wave eight is complete: migrations 087 to 097 are live; 098 is next.

- **WS-R39, the follower's own page (migration 101, 2026-09-04)** — controls used to be scattered through the Room (memory consent only settable at join, channels in `CheckinsPanel`, export/forget in `DataMenu`, locale in the header, subscription state nowhere a follower could act on it, WS-R30's own `cap_reached` offer never rendered at all - that workstream's own report named the gap). Migration 101 adds `vy_room_follower.settings_reviewed_at` (nullable timestamptz, one column, no new table, so no `PERSON_TABLES`/erasure/`relcheck` change - the row it lives on is already reached by `roomForget`'s existing delete). `api/_room-surface.js` gains `roomSettings` (one composed, session-scoped read: disclosure, follower state including the new column, push/WhatsApp/Telegram channel status re-derived as raw SQL rather than imported - both `_room-push.js` and `_room-whatsapp.js` already import FROM this file, so the reverse import would be circular, `decisions.md#ws-r39-room-settings-reused-raw-sql-not-imports` - the room's price, and any OPEN `cap_reached` offer) and `roomSettingsReviewed` (the one write, scoped off the session exactly as `roomSetLocale`'s is); `api/room.js` gains the `settings`/`settings_reviewed` ops, neither wired to `obsBestEffort` (law 5: a follower's settings visits are theirs). `src/room/AccountPage.tsx` is the new consolidated screen (memory toggle via the existing `join` op, channels via the existing push/WhatsApp/Telegram ops, subscription via the EXISTING `/api/room-pay {op:status}` read rather than a duplicate query, `decisions.md#ws-r39-subscription-status-read-through-existing-op-not-duplicated`, export/forget via the existing ops with WS-R27's receipt screen), reachable from a new header button; `DataMenu`/`CheckinsPanel` are deliberately left untouched rather than consolidated into it, a scoped-down decision logged with its own reversal condition (`decisions.md#ws-r39-account-page-additive-not-consolidating-scattered-controls`). The cap-reached offer card (RoomApp.tsx) needed a SECOND read after the refusal, since `room_free_cap_reached` throws before any response body could carry an offer (`decisions.md#ws-r39-cap-reached-offer-needs-a-second-read`); it renders only when `capped && capOffer` both hold, dismiss reuses the existing `roomDismissOffer` op, and the existing capped screen's own sentence is untouched, the card sitting under it. The quarterly reminder ("you have not looked at your settings since `<date>`") is a pure client-side computation with no server read and no analytics call, baselined on `settings_reviewed_at ?? joined_at` so a follower who has never opened the page still gets an honest date rather than the reminder never firing (`decisions.md#ws-r39-settings-reminder-baseline-includes-join-date`, `#ws-r39-settings-reminder-computed-client-side-no-analytics`). Two real defects found and fixed by the gates themselves: `.room-head-actions` had no `flex-wrap`, and the new header button pushed a real 5px horizontal overflow at 390px on the ORDINARY `room:talk` screen (not just the new `room:account` target) - confirmed as this workstream's own by reverting every tracked file to HEAD (`git checkout -- .`, never `git stash`) and re-running `check-layout.mjs` clean (638 blocks, 0 findings) before reapplying the patch and fixing it (`rejected.md#ws-r39-header-actions-row-overflowed-at-390px`); `evals/sqlcast.mjs` caught `roomSettingsReviewed`'s own `$4` bound to a `timestamptz` column with no cast, mirroring `roomSetLocale`'s neighbouring (safely uncast, `text`) statement without checking the column type first, fixed with `($4)::timestamptz` (`rejected.md#ws-r39-settings-reviewed-at-uncast-timestamp-param`). `scripts/check-layout.mjs` gains an `account` step on both the `room` and `room-hi` targets (`RoomApp.tsx`'s `fixtureAccountOpen`/`fixtureSettings`/`fixturePayment` props, `layoutFixture.tsx`'s `?screen=account`), and `evals/room-account/run.mjs` (new, registered in `evals/run.mjs`): 42/42 offline, $0 - the composed read's six sections, a two-follower world proving no channel/price/offer leak (price is a shared room fact and DOES appear for both, correctly), the reviewed write's session scope (proven against both followers' raw rows), the cap-reached offer recorded/surfaced/dismissed exactly once through the real `recordOffer`/`roomDismissOffer`, a static proof of `RoomApp.tsx`'s `capped && capOffer` guard, and both locales' key parity for the three new copy blocks; three negative controls (a body-supplied follower id silently ignored, a static message-column scan proven to bite on a poisoned copy, the copy gate catching a banned word and an em dash) — `measurements.md#ws-r39-room-account-offline-eval-2026-09-04`. `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **16/16 after** every file in this report, without `NEON_URL`; **no separate full untouched-tree run of the whole gate was captured at this session's start**, stated honestly rather than assumed (a narrower, direct untouched-tree check was made for the one regression this session found and fixed, and every other suite this report names was re-run by name after the changes and matched its own prior count) — `measurements.md#ws-r39-gate-2026-09-04`. `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls, unchanged. `npx tsc -b`: clean. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1043 nodes / 1276 edges. No new env vars. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: migration 101's one statement has never run against a live Postgres (no `NEON_URL` in this environment; the statement is listed verbatim in this workstream's final report for the main loop to `EXPLAIN`); no real `vy_room_follower.settings_reviewed_at` value exists outside a fake `db`; no real follower has ever seen the account page, the cap-reached card, or the quarterly reminder in a browser; the push-notification enable flow inside `AccountPage.tsx` (real `PushManager`/`Notification` browser APIs) has no offline eval of its own, the same gap `CheckinsPanel.tsx`'s own equivalent control has always had. Did not push; on branch `ws-r39-follower-account`.

**2026-09-04, WS-R39 merged (the follower's account page and the cap-reached card).** First of wave nine, merged over the wave-eight tip 170cb1e with no conflicts (the other three worktrees had not landed). Migration 101 is one nullable column on `vy_room_follower`, applied live and read back; the composed settings read and the reviewed write were EXPLAINed with typed literals and every plan sits on an index (`measurements.md#rooms-migration-101-live-verification-2026-09-04`). The page reads through existing ops and writes one timestamp scoped off the session; the cap-reached card renders only when both the refusal and WS-R30's `cap_reached` offer row exist and dismiss reuses the existing offer outcome; the quarterly reminder sentence is computed client-side from `settings_reviewed_at ?? joined_at` with no analytics. One real 390 px overflow in the Room header row was caught against the untouched tree and fixed in `room.css`; one uncast timestamp parameter was caught by sqlcast. Gate 16/16 on the merged tree (`EXIT 0`, layout 698 blocks judged including the two new `account` targets); room-account 42/42, room-leak 78/78, sqlcast 0 uncast. R36 (098), R37 (099) and R38 (100 if needed) remain in flight.

- **WS-R36, creator payouts as a product (migration 098, 2026-09-04)** — the payout builder (WS-R11) had no statement a creator could read, no payout that reached anywhere, and Suite seat revenue was logged as "not distributed to creators" (WS-R33's own open item); this workstream builds all three. Migration 098 widens `vy_creator_payout` (`suite_share_inr`, `provider_payout_ref`, the `state` CHECK from a two-value placeholder to the real closed set `built -> pending_account | queued -> sent -> settled | failed`, two new indexes) and adds `vy_creator_payout_account` (owner lane: `owner_user_id`, `provider`, `fund_account_ref`, `verified_at` - a provider-issued reference, never a bank detail). `api/_payments.js`'s `runPayoutRollup` folds a Suite seat share into gross as a FLAT `SUITE_SEAT_SHARE_BP` (50%, an operator placeholder) of the Suite's own per-seat price for every Room attached to a paying Suite at build time - never a re-derivation of what the Suite's ledger actually collected (`context/decisions.md#ws-r36-suite-share-flat-per-seat-not-ledger-derived`, citing WS-R33's own reversal condition), with the algebra worked out in the function's own comment so `gross = take + tds + net` holds by construction whether or not an owner has a Suite at all. The state machine (`sendPayout`, `markPayoutSent`, `markPayoutSettled`, `retryFailedPayout`) is a closed set, one transition each, every arrow a single UPDATE whose WHERE names the state(s) it leaves; `pending_account` is re-attempted by calling `sendPayout` again rather than a second operator-only unlock (`decisions.md#ws-r36-pending-account-reattempted-via-sendpayout-not-a-second-operator-unlock`); `failed` is retried ONLY by a new operator op (`api/payments.js`'s `retry_failed_payout`, 404 by name unless `OPS_OWNER_USER_IDS`, `api/_ops.js`'s own gate precedent). The fund account seam (`registerFundAccount` on both provider twins) VERIFIES a reference the owner already obtained from the provider's own onboarding, never CREATES one via a call that would need a bank detail as an argument (`decisions.md#ws-r36-fund-account-ref-verified-not-created-by-the-provider-seam`); `razorpay.js`'s `sendPayout`/`registerFundAccount` are marked NOT VERIFIED per that file's own convention, and RazorpayX's `POST /v1/payouts` uses the PLATFORM's own account number, never a creator's. The statement (`payoutStatementFromRows`/`payoutStatement`) carries the four numbers, the period, a `count(distinct subscription_id)` follower count, the Suite line and name, and the TDS disclosure sentence (a named constant, `TDS_DISCLOSURE_SENTENCE`, naming Section 194J as the operator's own unconfirmed guess and that an accountant must confirm it before any real payout) - nothing per follower, proven by a static source scan finding no follower identifier anywhere in the builder. `PayoutsCard.tsx` (self-contained, `CheckinsCard.tsx`'s own precedent) mounts in `RoomStudio.tsx` with the fund-account field, the period list, one open statement at a time, and JSON/plain-text downloads built in the browser from the server's own JSON - `RoomApp.tsx`'s export download's own in-page blob pattern. `evals/payouts/run.mjs`: 50/50 offline (the arithmetic across three owner shapes - follower-only, Suite-only with zero follower revenue, both - the full state machine including two required negative controls, the seam twins, the statement's own required negative control); `evals/payments/run.mjs`'s own `runPayoutRollup` fixture updated for the widened SQL, 62/62 unchanged; `evals/org-billing/run.mjs` 40/40 unchanged. One real regression caught by the gate itself and fixed, not worked around: `evals/studio-shell/run.mjs`'s orphan check failed on `PayoutsCard.tsx` (mounted inside `RoomStudio.tsx`, not the shell's own tab system) exactly the way `CheckinsCard.tsx`/`HandoffCard.tsx`/`SuiteCard.tsx` already needed the same fix for - added to that check's own named exclusion set (`context/rejected.md#ws-r36-new-self-contained-card-tripped-the-studio-shell-orphan-check`). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **16/16 on the untouched tree and 16/16 after** (one intermediate run hit a `layout readability` `EADDRINUSE:8931` from a concurrent sibling's own gate, confirmed environmental by re-running `check-layout.mjs` alone after the port freed). `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls, unchanged. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1040 nodes / 1276 edges. `node evals/sqlcast.mjs`: 166 tables (+1), 0 conflicts, 0 uncast sites. `node evals/persontables.mjs`: 133 person-keyed tables (74 owner lane, +1 - `vy_creator_payout_account`), 56 manifest entries unchanged (deliberately not in `PERSON_TABLES`, `vy_creator_payout`'s own precedent). `node evals/room-leak/run.mjs`: 78/78 unchanged. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: no statement in migration 098 or any function this workstream added has ever run against a live Postgres (no `NEON_URL` in this environment; every new SQL statement is listed verbatim in this workstream's final report for the main loop to `EXPLAIN`); no real `vy_creator_payout_account` row and no real `sent`/`settled`/`failed` payout exist anywhere outside a fake `db`; no human has ever seen `PayoutsCard.tsx` render in a browser; `scripts/relcheck.mjs` did not run (no `NEON_URL`); no live trigger (a RazorpayX payout-status webhook or poll) calls `markPayoutSent`/`markPayoutSettled` - both are proven by the offline suite and named as Phase 2 work, not silently missing. Did not push; on branch `ws-r36-payouts`.

**2026-09-04, WS-R36 merged (creator payouts and statements).** Second of wave nine, merged over the WS-R39 tip ab131c2. Conflicts only in the context files (unioned from the wave-eight base 170cb1e, 0 duplicated headings), the graph (+8 nodes, +7 edges) and the eval registry (both kept). Migration 098 was applied live in one transaction of 17 statements over an empty payout table (the old `pending`/`paid` state set and default had no remaining writer) and read back; every statement of the widened rollup, the closed state machine, the fund-account seam and the statement reads was EXPLAINed with typed literals (`measurements.md#rooms-migration-098-live-verification-2026-09-04`); the one sequential scan is the rollup's Suite arm over a tiny `vy_room` where 091's partial index already exists. A payout now carries the Suite seat share as a term in gross (a flat share of the per-seat price, not the Suite's collected ledger, with its reversal condition logged), moves through `built -> pending_account | queued -> sent -> settled | failed` with the leaving state in every UPDATE's WHERE, keeps only the provider-issued fund-account reference and never a bank detail, and produces a statement with the four numbers, the Suite line and the TDS disclosure sentence and nothing per follower. The first gate run lost the layout check to a sibling worktree on port 8931; rerun alone 16/16 (`EXIT 0`); payouts 50/50, payments 62/62, room-leak 78/78, replica erasure 20/20, sqlcast 0 uncast, persontables 56 entries. Still open: no trigger calls `markPayoutSent`/`markPayoutSettled` (a RazorpayX status webhook or poll is Phase 2), `SUITE_SEAT_SHARE_BP` is a placeholder against the price, the TDS rate is 0 bp pending an accountant, and the razorpay twin's two new calls are NOT VERIFIED (WS-R41). R37 (099) and R38 (100 if needed) remain in flight.

- **WS-R37, renewals and reminders (2026-09-04)** - "renewed unasked" becomes a real number and a reminder becomes a row before it is a message. Migration 099 adds `vy_renewal_reminder` (one table, three mutually exclusive subject-kind lanes - follower/creator/org, `vy_payment_event_one_lane`'s own shape restated for a delete/read predicate instead of a payment split; person lane for followers with `wipeWhere: "subject_kind = 'follower'"`, owner lane for creators reached by name in `api/_replica-full-erasure.js`, cascade-only for a Suite's own rows on `vy_org_subscription`'s own precedent) plus a `cancel_at_period_end` boolean added to all three subscription tables (078/091/095), deliberately separate from `state` so cancelling never cuts access early. `api/_renewals.js` holds every decision: `dueReminders` (three statements, one per subject kind, `NOT EXISTS` inside the WHERE never the SELECT list, `ws-r12-retention-exists-in-select-broke-the-leak-batterys-parser`'s lesson applied on arrival - and the file needed no `AGGREGATE_ONLY`/`ALLOWED` admission in `evals/room-leak/run.mjs` at all, since none of its statements ever names the two guarded tables, a real finding worth restating: the battery's own scan is over the FILE'S SOURCE TEXT including comments, and this workstream's own explanatory prose tripped it twice before being reworded, logged as its own rejection); `recordAndSend` (INSERT is the idempotency, `on conflict... do nothing`, send only on a fresh insert, a failed send leaves the row with `sent_at` null and a short code in `reason`, never retried); `sweep` (in-app always attempted, web push and Telegram for followers only where a pointer already exists via `api/_room-push.js`/`api/_room-surface.js`, reused verbatim); `cancelFollowerRenewal`/`cancelCreatorRenewal`/`cancelOrgRenewal` (per subject kind, through the SAME payments provider seam `api/_payments.js` already built - `providerFor`/`providerSecrets`/`paidSessionScope`/`orgAdminOrThrow` all exported from that file for reuse rather than re-derived); `renewedUnaskedCount` (the wired read: a creator subscription whose `current_period_start` advanced past its own `created_at` - a real prior period existed - with no `sent_at`-carrying `in_app` reminder row for that boundary as `period_end`, a plain LEFT JOIN rather than the EXISTS-in-select-list shape that broke WS-R12's own parser; gated on `vy_renewal_reminder` being applied so a database without migration 099 keeps the honest, unwired zero). `api/_phase-gate.js`'s `renewedUnasked` is now a thin call-through, `n` changed from `creators_total` to `renewed_total` (a creator who has never reached a second period should not count toward enough-data, logged as its own decision superseding `ws-r30-renewed-unasked-honest-zero`). `api/renewals-sweep.js` on a new daily cron (`0 */24 * * *`, picked over `0 6 * * *` specifically because `api/_sweep-schedule.js`'s own interval parser only recognises every-N-minutes/every-N-hours/hourly/one-weekly-slot shapes, not an arbitrary fixed hour - a daily-at-midnight cron in the every-N-hours shape is both a real, correct schedule AND a shape the ops board's own staleness math can read); the ops board's sweeps strip and the Phase gate card's own note line needed ZERO code changes, both already fully data-driven from `vercel.json`/the card's own `note` field. Cancel wired to three HTTP doors: `api/room-pay.js` (`op:"cancel"`, alongside its own `subscribe`/`status` - not `api/room.js` as the brief's file list named, logged as a deliberate substitution with its own decision), `api/payments.js` (`op:"cancel_creator_subscription"`), `api/org.js` (`op:"cancel_subscription"`). Follower UI: a new self-contained `SubscriptionPanel.tsx` (`CheckinsPanel.tsx`'s own dialog shape) opened from a new header button whenever `tier==='paid'`, reading a widened `followerSubscriptionStatus` (now also returns the room's current price and `cancel_at_period_end`) and a new `roomPayApi.ts` `cancelSubscription`; `src/room/copy.ts` gained a `subscription` block in both locales (title/tier lines/renewal sentence/cancel confirm-cancel-done, no urgency, no discount, no countdown) and a `withDate` helper beside the existing `withPrice`. Studio UI: `RoomStudio.tsx`'s tier sentence gained the renewal line and a Cancel button (`readCreatorTier`/`orgSubscriptionStatus` both widened by one column), `SuiteCard.tsx`'s money panel gained the same for a Suite's own seat subscription. `evals/renewals/run.mjs` (new, registered in `evals/run.mjs`): 52/52, 7 sections, three required negative controls (a second same-day sweep inserts and sends nothing; a cancelled or cancel-flagged subscription is excluded from every due-select; a static scan proving the module's own source and the push payload builder carry no follower-authored text). `api/_renewals.js`/`api/renewals-sweep.js` added to `evals/sqlcast/surface.mjs`'s strict-cast list from their first commit; `evals/sqlcast.mjs` 0 uncast sites. `api/memory.js`'s `PERSON_TABLES`/`REPLICA_PERSON_TABLES`, `evals/recall/run.mjs`'s FATE map ("forget-only"), `api/_room-surface.js`'s `ROOM_EXPORT_EXTRA` (count shape) and `roomForget`'s own explicit, child-before-parent delete statement were all updated in the same change - `evals/room-export/run.mjs`'s static layer 1 completeness check covers the new table by construction; its dynamic layer 2 world was not extended to seed it (named, not silently skipped). Two real defects found and fixed by the gates themselves before any count was recorded, both the SAME defect shape hit a third and fourth time this session: a backtick-quoted identifier inside a SQL comment inside a JS template literal closed the string early in `api/_replica-full-erasure.js`'s new CTE (caught by `node --check`, `rejected.md#ws-r37-sql-comment-backticks-terminate-the-template-literal-a-third-time`), and this workstream's own explanatory prose named the leak battery's two guarded table names while explaining it never touches them, tripping the battery's own prose-not-only-SQL scan (`rejected.md#ws-r37-explanatory-comments-named-the-guarded-tables-and-tripped-the-leak-battery`). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **16/16 on the untouched tree** (confirmed first via a WIP-commit-and-`git reset --hard`/`--soft` round trip) and **16/16 after** every file in this entry (`measurements.md#ws-r37-renewals-gate-results-2026-09-04`). `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1043 nodes / 1280 edges. `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls, unchanged. No new env vars - `ROOM_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`/`ROOM_PUSH_VAPID_*`/`PAYMENTS_*` are all reused, not invented. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: no statement in migration 099 or any function this workstream added has ever run against a live Postgres (no `NEON_URL` in this environment; every new SQL statement is listed verbatim in this workstream's final report for the main loop to `EXPLAIN`); no real `vy_renewal_reminder` row and no real `cancel_at_period_end` value exist anywhere outside a fake `db`; no reminder has ever reached a real Telegram chat, a real push subscription, or a real creator's studio screen; no human has ever seen `SubscriptionPanel.tsx`, the studio's cancel controls, or the Phase gate card's new note text render in a browser; the daily cron has never fired against a real deployment; `scripts/relcheck.mjs` did not run (no `NEON_URL`). Migration 099 is the last of wave nine's own reserved range (098-101); 102 is next. Did not push; on branch `ws-r37-renewals`.

**2026-09-04, WS-R37 merged (renewals and reminders).** Third of wave nine, merged over the WS-R36 tip 072cd26. Conflicts in the context files (unioned from the wave-eight base 170cb1e, 0 duplicated headings), the graph (+11 nodes, +11 edges), the schema (099 placed before 101), the eval registry, the payments door and the studio payments client (both sides kept, one closing brace restored where each hunk began mid-statement). Migration 099 was applied live in one transaction of 16 statements and read back (`measurements.md#rooms-migration-099-live-verification-2026-09-04`). The live EXPLAIN found two things the fake db could not: the follower due-select read `r.locale` from `vy_room`, which has no locale (a follower's lives on `vy_room_follower`, WS-R24), fixed by joining the follower row for that one column with `api/_renewals.js` admitted to the leak battery for the one-row-back-to-its-own-follower shape and three tighter negative controls replacing the workstream's absence claim (`rejected.md#ws-r37-room-locale-does-not-exist-the-fake-db-passed-it`); and the two `recordAndSend` updates by `reminder_id` seq-scanned against the composite primary key, fixed with a unique index added to 099 and applied live. A third defect surfaced at the merge gate: R39's Room layout fixture built a payment status without R37's `price_inr`/`currency`, caught by tsc and fixed with the same honest nulls the API returns. A reminder is now a ledger row before it is a message (one per subject per period per channel, the INSERT the idempotency), the sweep's due-select carries the NOT EXISTS inside and runs daily, every subject kind can cancel at period end through the seam with `cancel_at_period_end` distinct from state, and the Phase gate's "renewed unasked" reads the real `vy_creator_subscription` joined to the reminder table. Gate 16/16 on the merged tree after the fixes (`EXIT 0`); renewals 54/54, room-leak 78/78, phase-gate 50/50, payments 62/62, org 54/54, payouts 50/50, room-export ok, persontables 57 entries, replica erasure 20/20, room-account 42/42. R38 (no migration) is last.

- **WS-R38, the door battery: the 17th named release gate (2026-09-04).** The leak battery (WS-R8) proves the three scopes hold for a WELL-BEHAVED client; nothing before this workstream attacked the doors themselves. `evals/room-doors/run.mjs` now does, through the REAL decision modules the thin HTTP doors call (never a re-implemented check): forged/expired sessions (a session minted by the real `mintRoomSession` with the fixture's own secret, then tampered — wrong signature, wrong secret, a payload edit that breaks the signature, 13 hours stale), cross-Room sessions (an internally-consistent-looking session renamed to a different room's slug, or carrying a mismatched room/agent id claim, or a stale disclosure digest), body-supplied ids belonging to another follower (a `handoff_id`/`checkin_id` in the body, always ignored in favour of the session's own scope), webhook replay and tampered signatures (`payments-webhook.js`'s follower lane: a signed event applied once, replayed as a no-op with the ledger byte-for-byte unchanged; a signature over a modified body refused; `room-tg.js`'s header secret; `room-wa.js`'s HMAC via `api/whatsapp.js`'s own `signatureOk`), an owner bearer reaching for another owner's replica or org (`replica.js`, `room-publish.js`, `checkins.js`, `handoff.js`, `org.js` — the last confirming this codebase's own "404, never 403" law: a non-member gets `org_not_found`, never a roster), rate-key malformation (an empty key, a 10,000-character key, two textual spellings of one IPv6 address — all bounded, the IPv6 case measured and left uncanonicalized rather than fixed with no demonstrated attacker path, `decisions.md#ws-r38-ipv6-key-canonicalization-not-fixed`), invite-code guessing (five wrong guesses refused by name, the real code still redeems, the door's own 60/min in-memory scope bounds a guessing script), and the OTP verify brute-force floor (re-asserting WS-R32's own 10-a-minute ceiling). 109 assertions, 15 doors (enumerated by a static rule over `api/*.js`'s own source — reads a request body AND imports one of 14 named Room/owner-door decision modules, or is `api/account.js` by name — asserted complete against `api/`'s directory listing; `api/export.js`/`api/memory.js` deliberately excluded as Meera's own already-battery-tested account-wide surfaces, `decisions.md#ws-r38-door-list-completeness-rule`), offline, $0, ~1.8s (`measurements.md#ws-r38-door-battery-case-counts-2026-09-04`). Building it found and fixed two real findings, each its own commit: the 12-hour session TTL (`ROOM_SESSION_TTL_MS`) was enforced by only 4 of roughly 11 session-consuming scope resolvers — `roomSay`/`roomSpeak`/`roomSetLocale`/`_payments.js`'s `paidSessionScope` had it, `_room-surface.js`'s `selfScope` (the gate for `export`/`forget`/`offer_dismiss`, the two ops this file's own header names as the highest-consequence a stolen session can reach), `followerHistory`, `roomCitations` (which also never checked a follower row existed at all), and the independently-copied `followerScope` in `_handoff.js`/`_checkins.js`/`_room-push.js`/`_room-whatsapp.js` did not — fixed with one shared, exported `assertSessionFresh(payload, now)` now called from every one of them (`rejected.md#ws-r38-session-ttl-missing-from-most-followerscope-copies`, `decisions.md#ws-r38-assert-session-fresh-shared-helper`). Second: `api/room.js`'s `"thread"` op called the bare `createThread` primitive straight off a decoded session with no check that a live, attested follower row still existed — a session whose follower row `roomForget` had already deleted could still mint an orphan `vy_room_thread` row — fixed with `createFollowerThread`, which runs `selfScope`'s own three checks first; `createThread` itself and every other caller (evals, `_handoff.js`, `_pulse.js`) are unchanged (`rejected.md#ws-r38-thread-op-no-live-follower-check`). Both fixes proven not just dynamically but by static wiring proofs (§9, mirroring `evals/rate-limit/run.mjs`'s own §7 method) confirming the real door and every `followerScope` copy actually calls the fixed function — verified by temporarily reverting each fix in turn and confirming the corresponding case (and, for the thread door, the §9 wiring proof) fails. A third attempt — a symmetric lower bound on `assertSessionFresh` to also refuse a future-dated `iat` — was tried and reverted: it broke a repo-wide eval convention (a session minted against real time, read against an unrelated fixed scenario clock for business-logic math), and no request field ever reaches the `now` a session is minted with in this product, so a future `iat` is not a live external hole today; measured and documented rather than fixed blind (`rejected.md#ws-r38-session-clock-skew-lower-bound`). No migration: every finding was a missing check in existing JS, never a schema change, so 100 stays unused and 100 is still the next free number. `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **16/16 on the untouched tree** (`git checkout -- .` back to `170cb1e`, confirmed before any file in this workstream was written) and **17/17 after** (the "room door battery" gate), both without `NEON_URL`; the layout gate hit a sibling worktree's `EADDRINUSE:8931` twice and was reconfirmed standalone both times once the port freed. `node evals/run.mjs`: every suite including the new `room-doors`, 0 failures. `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1039 nodes / 1275 edges. `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` updated together with the new gate count (17 without `NEON_URL`, 19 with it). NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: nothing in this workstream touched a live database (no `NEON_URL` in this environment, and none of its fixes needed one); no real session, follower, replica, org, invite or webhook event from this workstream's fixtures exists outside a fake `db`; no human has exercised any of these doors in a browser. Did not push; on branch `ws-r38-door-battery`.

**2026-09-04, after wave nine: the renewals cron was not a cron.** Vercel refused both projects' deployments of the wave-nine branch: `0 */24 * * *` (WS-R37's daily sweep, written that way so the ops board's interval parser could read it) fails cron validation, an hour step must be 1..23. Fixed as `0 0 * * *` with the parser taught the daily slot shape and four ops assertions added, one of which checks every step in `vercel.json` against the platform's range (`rejected.md#ws-r37-cron-step-of-24-hours-is-not-a-cron`). **2026-09-04, WS-R46, the Room on a creator's own site (no migration).** One script tag, one button: `<script src=".../room-embed.js" data-room="<slug>" defer></script>` renders the creator's display name plus "AI", the server's own three-line disclosure card underneath, and "Powered by Vyakti", and opens `/r/<slug>?via=embed` in a new tab on click. `api/room-embed.js` (the thin HTTP door, GET only, unauthenticated, `?slug=` branch cached `public, max-age=300` and the bare script cached `public, max-age=3600`) sits over `api/_room-embed.js` (every decision: the pure JSON builder `buildRoomEmbedJson`, the one database read `readRoomEmbed`, and the hand-written `ROOM_EMBED_JS` string), wired into `vercel.json`'s `/room-embed.js` rewrite. The read reuses `api/_room-surface.js`'s `resolveRoom` verbatim rather than `api/_room-publish.js`'s owner-scoped `ownedRoomRow` (the brief's own named precedent, which cannot answer an anonymous visitor at all) — a deliberate substitution, logged with its own decision, matching the shape of WS-R37's own substitution one workstream over. An unpublished Room and an unknown slug answer the byte-identical `{room:null}`, inherited for free from `resolveRoom`'s own WHERE clause rather than built here. v0 never frames the Room (no iframe, no per-creator allowed-origin table); the reversal condition is the first creator who asks for the button to sit inside their page rather than beside it. `src/studio/RoomStudio.tsx` gained an "On your own site" card (the snippet, a copy control, plain English copy matching every other card on that screen) built from the new `roomEmbedSnippet` in `roomPublishApi.ts`, `channelsApi.ts`'s own `embedSnippet` shape one surface over. `evals/room-embed/run.mjs` (51/51, registered in `evals/run.mjs`) drives the shipped `ROOM_EMBED_JS` for real with `new Function` against a hand-rolled DOM fake (no jsdom): a published Room renders the button, the exact server disclosure text, and the `?via=embed` link with `rel="noopener noreferrer"`; a missing `data-room`, a paused Room, a never-published Room, an unknown slug and a network failure all remove the script's own tag; the JSON builder is proven pure and follower-blind; three negative controls (a second fetch target, a follower-table reference, an em dash via the real `scripts/check-copy.mjs` scanner) each prove the corresponding check actually bites. Script size measured on every run: 2,539 raw bytes, 1,677 minified (`npx esbuild --minify`), under the 6,144-byte cap. `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **17/17 on the untouched tree at 321a0fd** (confirmed via a `git reset --hard` round trip, never `git stash`) and **17/17 after** (unchanged count — no new named gate; the suite lands inside the existing "eval suite" check). `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls, unchanged. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1081 nodes / 1321 edges. No new SQL statement (the one read is a call-through to `resolveRoom`, already EXPLAINed by earlier workstreams) and no new env var. Did not push; on branch `ws-r46-room-embed`. NOT PROVEN: nothing in this workstream has ever run against a live Postgres or a live Vercel deployment; no real creator has pasted the snippet onto a real page; `?via=embed` has never actually reached WS-R40's arrival counter outside this description of the contract; `scripts/relcheck.mjs` did not run (no `NEON_URL`).

**2026-09-04, WS-R46 merged (the Room on the creator's own site).** First of wave ten, merged over the cron-fix tip 321a0fd with no conflicts. **2026-09-04, WS-R50: accessibility as a gate, the 18th named release gate.** Nothing before this workstream checked the Room or the studio shell for anything beyond `check-layout.mjs`'s own readability floor and DESIGN-LAW's tap-target size. `scripts/check-accessibility.mjs` now does: axe-core (WCAG 2.1 A/AA tags) in a real Chromium on `127.0.0.1:8933` (the layout gate owns 8931, WS-R49's performance gate 8932), against every `room`/`room-hi` screen (join/talk/account, reusing `check-layout.mjs`'s own fixture query shapes rather than a second fixture), the studio shell's three tabs, `/` and `/vyakti` (13 pages), plus `room:talk` once each under `reducedMotion`/`forcedColors` emulation — and a hand-written keyboard walk axe cannot do itself, driving real Tab/Enter/Space/Escape against `room:talk` and `room:account`. BEFORE, on the untouched tree: 1 serious axe violation (`color-contrast`, six `--ink-faint` selectors on the studio shell's "meet" tab, 3.47:1-4.11:1 against a 4.5:1 floor — fixed with a new, narrowly-scoped `--ink-faint-aa` token rather than recolouring the 50+ other places the shared token is used but this workstream's targets never proved failing, `rejected.md#ws-r50-ink-faint-token-wide-recolour`) and 5 keyboard findings: Tab order moved backward on both screens (root cause: `RoomApp.tsx`'s scroll-to-bottom effect firing on the very first mount, before any interaction — a keyboard user's first Tab landed on the composer at the foot of the screen instead of the language switch at the top; fixed by skipping exactly that one firing, `decisions.md#ws-r50-scroll-to-bottom-skips-the-first-mount`), Escape closed nothing (none of the Room's five `role="dialog"` panels — the data menu, the account page, check-ins, subscription, handoff — had an Escape handler at all, unlike every other modal in this codebase; all five fixed with the same `AuthSheet.tsx`-precedent pattern plus `aria-modal="true"`), and one activation finding that was itself a false positive in its first form: reading `aria-pressed` after a keyboard Space press on the pulse toggle failed identically whether or not the control was keyboard-reachable, because `room-layout-fixture.html` stubs no `/api/*` route at all and the toggle's real action is a live fetch — proven with a plain mouse click on the same button in the same fixture, also silently failing (`rejected.md#ws-r50-pulse-toggle-aria-pressed-false-positive`). Fixing it properly surfaced a much wider defect: 18 buttons across `RoomApp.tsx` (7) and `AccountPage.tsx` (11, every control on the page) fired their action from `onPointerDown` alone with no `onClick`, which native keyboard activation never dispatches — `AccountPage.tsx` (WS-R39) had drifted from its three sibling dialogs, which all use `onClick` throughout (`rejected.md#ws-r50-onpointerdown-only-breaks-keyboard-activation`); fixed by adding a matching `onKeyDown` (the new exported `activateOnKey` helper) beside every existing `onPointerDown`, keeping the pointer-feedback timing intact. Also fixed, found by direct calculation rather than axe (WCAG 1.4.11 is not in the WCAG21AA tag set axe scores by default): the Room's keyboard focus ring was falling through to `studio.css`'s pre-WS-R31 weak base-layer rule (~1.9:1, the same one already fixed for `.studio-shell` specifically) because the Room shares that CSS layer but carries no `.studio-shell` class — fixed with the identical opaque `--forest` ring tokens.css already defines (`decisions.md#ws-r50-room-focus-ring-contrast`). AFTER: 0 critical/serious/moderate/minor axe violations across all 13 pages, 0 keyboard findings on either screen (including a new activation proof this workstream added for the account page's own "Close" button, with a working negative control), runtime ~30s, comfortably under the brief's 3-minute ceiling (`measurements.md#ws-r50-accessibility-before-after`). Threshold: `serious`/`critical` blocks, `moderate`/`minor` reports only (`decisions.md#ws-r50-accessibility-impact-threshold`). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **17/17 on the untouched tree** and **18/18 after** (the new `accessibility` gate), both without `NEON_URL` — two sibling workstreams (WS-R42, WS-R49) were adding a gate each at the same time this count was taken, so this is what this workstream observed plus its own gate, and the main loop reconciles the final number at the merge. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1082 nodes / 1322 edges. `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` updated with the gate and the observed count. No migration, no new env var. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: this is Chromium's own accessibility tree and a synthetic keyboard driver, not a real screen reader — no NVDA, JAWS or VoiceOver session has read any of these screens aloud, and the "focus follows visual order" claim rests on this codebase having no positive `tabindex` anywhere (checked) rather than on a person confirming the reading order sounds right. Did not push; on branch `ws-r50-accessibility`.

**WS-R49, performance budgets for a mid-range Android on a 4G day (2026-09-04).** Nothing in this repo measured what a follower actually waits for; this workstream builds that measurement, sets budgets, and fixes what fails. `scripts/check-performance.mjs` renders `/`, `/vyakti`, `/r/<slug>` (via `room-layout-fixture.html?screen=join`, the same signed-in-without-a-secret technique the layout gate uses) and `/studio` signed out, in real Chromium at 390x844 under CDP throttling (CPU 4x, 1.6Mbps/750Kbps/150ms — the long-standing Chrome DevTools/Lighthouse "Fast 3G" preset, cited as such rather than invented) with a named budget table (LCP <= 2500ms, CLS <= 0.1, TBT <= 300ms, JS <= 180KB, font <= 120KB, no render-blocking third party), n=3 cold-cache runs, median. The gate's own first draft served raw uncompressed bytes and reported `/r/<slug>` and `/studio` both failing badly on numbers no real Vercel deployment produces; fixed to gzip compressible responses and read CDP's real `encodedDataLength` (`rejected.md#ws-r49-performance-gate-served-uncompressed-bytes`) — the Room needed **zero product code changes** once measured correctly (79.7KB JS, well under budget). `/studio`'s failure was real: `StudioApp.tsx` statically imported all ~27 wizard panels regardless of sign-in state, so a signed-out visit downloaded the whole authenticated wizard before ever showing a sign-in form. Nine panels (ReviewQueue plus the eight heaviest by source size) converted to `React.lazy` + `Suspense` at their real usage sites; `/studio` JS transfer 195.2KB -> 137.5KB (29.6% reduction), now under the 180KB budget (`measurements.md#ws-r49-studio-lazy-panels-2026-09-04`). Font budget is 0KB everywhere by construction: this repo loads no web font anywhere, Devanagari included (system font stack only). One real regression caught and fixed by the gates themselves: `evals/studio-shell/run.mjs`'s orphan check's regex only recognized a static `import X from "./X"` and reported all nine lazy panels orphaned; widened to also match `import("./X")` rather than adding real, still-mounted panels to the check's exclusion set (`rejected.md#ws-r49-studio-shell-orphan-check-dynamic-import-gap`). A deliberately-not-fixed item, logged with its reversal condition: the Room's `main.tsx` still imports the whole 4,209-line `studio.css` for its palette (~27.9KB gzip of waste), left alone because no CSS budget exists, every measured metric already passes, and the fix would touch this repo's most contended, cascade-layer-bug-prone file (`decisions.md#ws-r49-room-css-palette-import-not-restructured`). Wired as the 18th named gate in `scripts/verify-release.mjs` (was 17; CLAUDE.md and AGENTS.md updated together, noting WS-R42/WS-R50 are each adding a gate in the same wave and the main loop reconciles the true count at the merge). Gate 17/17 on the untouched tree (one `EADDRINUSE:8931` collision, reconfirmed standalone), 18/18 after; the new gate itself runs in 45.6s, under its own 3-minute ceiling; layout readability unchanged at 698 blocks judged, confirming the lazy-loading dropped no rendered content. No migration, no new env var. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: every number here is a CDP-throttled Chromium simulation on this machine's CPU, never a real mid-range Android device on a real Indian mobile network — the stated reversal condition for every budget is exactly that measurement disagreeing. Did not push; on branch `ws-r49-performance-budgets`.

`scripts/check-layout.mjs` gained `creators`/`creators-hi` targets; because the layout gate's own `vite build` step never runs `scripts/vercel-build.sh`'s copy step, `site/creators.html` was added as logged, harmless duplicate URL (`decisions.md#ws-r45-creators-html-vite-entry-for-gate-only`). `scripts/check-copy.mjs`'s `CODENAME_FILES`/`ROOMS_VOCAB_FILES` regexes now also cover scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **17/17 on the untouched tree** (confirmed by backing up and reverting the two files already in progress at session start, gating, then restoring them) and **17/17 after** every commit — the check COUNT did not move, since this workstream added targets/suites inside two EXISTING named gates (`layout readability`, `eval suite`) rather than a new named gate of its own. - **WS-R48, Suites sell themselves (migration 107, 2026-09-04)** - Suites (WS-R28's org, WS-R33's seat billing) existed as ops and a Studio card with no way for a coaching institute, a creator collective, an agency or a publisher to find them, understand the price, and start one without a person in the loop. This workstream builds the B2B front door. `site/suites.html` (new, self-contained, India-first: rupee prices, both locales via `?lang=hi`, `site/vyakti.html`'s own no-build-step convention restated) states who it is for, what a seat is, what a Suite admin sees (counts only), the disclosure a follower sees, and the two real prices - `SUITE_SEAT_PRICE_STARTER_INR`/`SUITE_SEAT_PRICE_INSTITUTE_INR` - mirrored from `api/_org.js` with a `// mirror of api/_org.js#NAME` marker comment WS-R42's own mirror gate can key on, never a second literal. Self-serve "Start a Suite": `src/studio/startSuiteDraft.ts` (new) captures a name/plan/seat-count draft into `localStorage` before React mounts (`main.tsx`, `studioAuth.ts`'s `restoreStudioMode()` precedent restated, since an OAuth redirect cannot be trusted to preserve extra query params), and `SuiteCard.tsx` (existing, unmodified write path) consumes it once mounted, calling the SAME `createSuite`/`startSuiteSubscription` the manual controls already call - no new HTTP door, no new backend function. With `PAYMENTS_PROVIDER=fake` this actually starts a subscription (proven by recomputing the fake provider's own deterministic reference from the price that reached it); with the `none` default it creates the org and gracefully reports that the subscription needs to be started manually once payments are configured, proven to make zero provider calls both dynamically (no `vy_org_subscription` row written) and by a static source-order proof. "Talk to us first" reuses the EXISTING `api/apply.js`/`api/_apply.js` door with a new `intent` column (migration 107, `not null default 'creator'`, `check (intent in ('creator','suite'))`) rather than overloading the existing free-text `audience` field. Migration 107 also adds `vy_room.org_attached_at` (written by `attachRoom`, cleared by `detachRoom`) since `vy_room.updated_at` is touched by publish/pause/price-change too and cannot honestly answer "attached this week". `api/_funnel.js` gains `suitesFunnelThisWeek` (Suites started / seats attached, rolling 7 days); `api/_apply.js` gains `suiteIntentApplicationsThisWeek`; `api/_ops.js` surfaces all three under a new `suites` field on the board, with no n>=5 floor (none of the three counts describes a follower, `whatsappSpendThisMonth`/`stalled_at`'s own floor-free precedent). `scripts/check-layout.mjs` gains a `suites` target (English/Hindi), the ONE target in that file serving its fixture straight from `site/` rather than `dist/`, since this page ships no build step by design; two real findings caught and fixed (a 2-column grid whose trailing text had no wrapping element for the gate's own child-counting heuristic to see, and `.hero p.fine` exceeding the character-per-line ceiling at tablet width with no `max-width`). `scripts/check-copy.mjs` gains `site/suites.html` to `CODENAME_FILES`/`ROOMS_VOCAB_FILES`. `evals/suites-self-serve/run.mjs` (new, registered): 60/60 offline - the price/seat-bound mirror parsed from both real files, the self-serve flow through the real seam, the apply intent, the rolling-window counts, and the three required negative controls (every currency-adjacent digit run on the page is a real constant; a seat count outside the real CHECK bounds extracted from `db/schema.sql` is refused by a database-CHECK-emulating fake db standalone, not only by the JS bound that also refuses it; a poisoned copy fixture fails the real copy gate and the real page scans clean). One real regression found and fixed by the gate itself, the SAME defect shape logged three times before this session: a new header comment named the leak battery's two guarded table names in prose, tripping its blunt scanner (`context/rejected.md#ws-r48-explanatory-comment-named-the-guarded-tables-a-fourth-time`). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **17/17 on the untouched tree** (an isolated `git worktree add --detach` clone at 321a0fd, never this session's own tree) **and 17/17 after** every file in this report, both confirmed via a standalone `check-layout.mjs` run once a concurrent sibling worktree's own `EADDRINUSE:8931` cleared. `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1084 nodes / 1324 edges. `node evals/sqlcast.mjs`: 167 tables (unchanged, no new table), 0 conflicts, 0 uncast sites. `node evals/persontables.mjs`: 134 person-keyed tables, 57 manifest entries (unchanged - neither new column is a person column). `node evals/room-leak/run.mjs`: 78/78 after the fix above. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: no statement in migration 107 has ever run against a live Postgres (no `NEON_URL` in this environment; both new statements are listed verbatim in this workstream's final report for the main loop to `EXPLAIN`); no real `vy_creator_application.intent` value and no real `vy_room.org_attached_at` value exist outside a fake `db`; no human has ever seen `site/suites.html` render in a browser, clicked "Start a Suite", or completed a real sign-in round trip through it; `scripts/relcheck.mjs` did not run (no `NEON_URL`). Did not push; on branch `ws-r48-suites-self-serve`. No new env vars.

**WS-R42, the money reconciles (migration 104, 2026-09-04).** WS-R33 logged that a creator-tier charge writes no ledger row; this workstream closes that gap and builds a reconciliation gate over the whole payments surface. The brief's own words ("under migration 095's two-lane CHECK") read, on a first pass, like an instruction to widen `vy_payment_event_one_lane` to a third disjunct - rejected before a line of SQL was written, the same conclusion migration 095's own header and `context/decisions.md#ws-r33-creator-tier-charge-has-no-ledger-row`'s own reversal condition already name: `platform_take_inr`/`creator_share_inr` exist to record a SPLIT, and a creator's own subscription to the platform has none. Built `vy_creator_charge_event` (migration 104) instead: a new, dedicated, owner-lane table, written inside `applyWebhook`'s creator lane in the SAME statement as the state flip (a single CTE: `sub_update` then a conditional `charge_insert`, idempotent on `(provider, provider_charge_ref)`), only for a landed-charge kind (`subscription.charged`/`subscription.activated`) with a positive amount - a pause, a cancellation or the first `authenticated` still flips state and writes nothing, exactly as before. A seat-covered creator's own charge attempt is refused before any provider call (WS-R33's own law, unchanged), so structurally zero rows can ever land for them. `reconcile` (api/_payments.js) is a pure function over rows: the follower lane checks a period's ledger sum against `gross_inr - suite_share_inr`; the Suite lane recomputes `runPayoutRollup`'s OWN flat per-seat formula from `suiteRows` rather than comparing against an org-lane ledger sum, which turned out NOT to be the invariant the builder actually holds (`context/decisions.md#ws-r42-suite-reconcile-recomputes-the-builder-formula`, citing `ws-r36-suite-share-flat-per-seat-not-ledger-derived`); the creator lane is reported, never compared, since it has no payout counterpart. Reading migration 078's own header rather than trusting the brief's assumption: both the ledger and every payout column are already whole rupees, so `reconcile` performs NO unit conversion (`context/decisions.md#ws-r42-ledger-and-payout-are-both-whole-rupees`) - a mismatch's `difference_paise` is the rupee difference times 100. An operator op (`reconcile`, 404 by name unless `OPS_OWNER_USER_IDS`) runs it over one period; the ops board gains a count of periods with findings. `scripts/check-mirrors.mjs` (new, the 20th named gate) parses every `// mirror of api/<file>.js#<NAME>` marker in `src/` and `site/suites.html` on both sides and fails on disagreement - the two Pulse constants (`PULSE_MAX_LABELS`/`PULSE_LABEL_MAX_LEN`) got the marker, and the five markers WS-R48 had already built in `site/suites.html` anticipating this gate by name were found clean (7 markers total, 0 disagree). `evals/payments-reconcile/run.mjs` (new, registered, 30/30): the consistent fixture (three Rooms, one attached to a paying Suite, two follower subscriptions each, one creator-tier charge, one seat-covered creator) finds zero mismatches; four negative controls (a removed ledger row names the Room and the paise difference; an unattached Room's `suite_share_inr` is a finding; the seat-covered creator writes structurally zero charge rows; `check-mirrors` catches a one-off fixture mismatch). Also run and unaffected: `evals/payments` 62/62, `evals/org-billing` 40/40, `evals/payouts` 50/50, `evals/renewals` 54/54, `evals/ops` 68/68, `evals/room-doors` 109/109; `evals/sqlcast.mjs` 168 tables (+1); `evals/persontables.mjs` 135 person-keyed, 75 owner-lane (+1, `vy_creator_charge_event` auto-classified, no manifest edit). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **19/19 on the untouched tree** (two `EADDRINUSE` collisions from a sibling worktree, both confirmed environmental standalone) and **20/20 after** (one `EADDRINUSE` collision, same cause, confirmed environmental standalone). `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1137 nodes / 1391 edges. Gate count moved to 20/22 in CLAUDE.md and AGENTS.md in the same commit as the gate itself, per the brief's own instruction. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: migration 104 has never run against a live Postgres (no `NEON_URL` in this environment; every new SQL statement is listed verbatim in this workstream's final report for the main loop to `EXPLAIN`); no real `vy_creator_charge_event` row exists outside a fake `db`; `reconcilePeriod`'s own four SELECTs have never executed live; Suite-lane reconciliation reads CURRENT attachment, not a historical snapshot of the period's own end, so an OLD period can report a false Suite finding if attachment changed since (`context/decisions.md#ws-r42-reconcile-suite-lane-uses-current-attachment`); `scripts/relcheck.mjs` did not run (no `NEON_URL`). Did not push; on branch `ws-r42-money-reconciles`. No new env vars.

**2026-09-04, WS-R41 (provider contracts).** Enumerated the 7 in-scope `NOT VERIFIED` marks in `api/whatsapp.js`, `api/tg.js` and `api/_payments/providers/razorpay.js` (the rest of the grep's ~60 hits are the unrelated `age_tier`/`unverified` states, out of scope) and checked each against the named provider's own document (`developers.facebook.com/docs/whatsapp` and `.../graph-api/webhooks`, `core.telegram.org/bots/api` and its changelog, `razorpay.com/docs`, `datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc8291`), all fetched 2026-09-04. Found and fixed two real bugs neither offline eval had ever caught, both because the marks they lived under said plainly that no real call had ever been made: `api/tg.js` was sending the pre-Bot-API-7.0 `reply_to_message_id` field (Telegram's own changelog: "replaced" by `reply_parameters` in Bot API 7.0, 2023-12-29) - every threaded room reply this file has ever built would reach a current Bot API server unthreaded; and `api/_push/webpush.js`'s `decryptPayload` required the `aes128gcm` header's `rs` field to equal the actual record length exactly, which RFC 8291 §4's own MUST forbids relying on (`rs` is a ceiling) and which rejected RFC 8291 Appendix A's own published vector once that vector was actually fed through the real encoder and decoder in `evals/room-push/run.mjs`'s new §7 - this workstream's law 2 (reproduce the RFC's exact bytes, not just round-trip against oneself) is what surfaced it; the round-trip eval that already existed never could, because it always drove both sides against this file's own single default. WhatsApp's send/verify/parse shapes and Telegram's call/response envelope and webhook-secret shape were confirmed correct and flipped to verified, with one near-miss caught by cross-checking two doc pages (a general WhatsApp reference page's own auto-summary put a reaction's `message_id` under the wrong field; the dedicated reaction-messages page corrected it, and the code was already right). RazorpayX's payout/fund-account ENTITY shapes verified field for field; the three OPERATION pages (subscription PATCH, fund-account GET, payout POST - method+path+parameter table) could not be reached despite many distinct URL guesses, which looks like a client-routed docs SPA rather than missing content, logged with every URL tried (`rejected.md#ws-r41-provider-docs-sites-resist-a-single-page-fetch-tool-two-ways`). `sendPayout`'s `reference_id` now enforces the doc's own 40-character ceiling. The two `bindWhatsappClone`/`bindTelegramClone` marks and `setMessageReaction`'s own body shape stay open by design (law 5): the first two are this platform's own operational state, not a fact any provider document can settle; the third is blocked by this session's fetch tool truncating Telegram's one giant reference page before reaching it. Gate 19/19 before and after (untouched tree matched the post-change tree's result); `evals/room-push/run.mjs` 52/52 (was 43/43 before §7), `evals/room-whatsapp/run.mjs` 68/68 (was 63/63), `evals/payments/run.mjs` 70/70 (was 62/62), `evals/payouts/run.mjs` 50/50 (unchanged count, one comment adjusted to keep a negative-control scanner passing on accurate quoted text). `evals/mp/tgbot.mjs`'s own updated assertion (matching the `reply_parameters` fix) needs `NEON_URL` and did not run. No migration; no new env vars. Did not push; on branch `ws-r41-provider-contracts`. Remaining in flight: R40, R42, R43, R44.

- **WS-R44, the door battery learns the ops that landed beside it (2026-09-04, no migration).** WS-R38's door battery named its cases by hand (say/thread/history/export/forget/citations/offer_dismiss); three waves since then (WS-R39's `settings`/`settings_reviewed`, WS-R37's `cancel`/`cancel_creator_subscription`/`cancel_subscription`, WS-R36's `payout_statements`/`payout_statement`/`register_fund_account`/`retry_failed_payout`, plus WS-R45's `list`/`unlist`/`set_bio` and WS-R47's `mine_issue`/`mine_list`) added ops to `room.js`/`room-pay.js`/`payments.js`/`org.js`/`room-publish.js`/`invites.js` covered only through a shared scope resolver, never a case of their own. `evals/room-doors/run.mjs` gains six new dynamic sections (§9-§14) casing every one of those ops through the real decision module (`selfScope`, `paidSessionScope`, `orgAdminOrThrow`, `assertOwnerScope`+its owner_user_id/replica_id WHERE, or a bearer-scoped quota), and its static wiring section (renamed §15) is extended to prove the class-b room-id-match check present in `roomSetLocale`/`roomSpeak` and in `_room-push.js`/`_room-whatsapp.js`/`_pulse.js`'s own `followerScope`, giving `room.js`'s `locale`/`speak`/`pulse_optin`/`pulse_revoke`/`push_*`/`whatsapp_*` ops their own coverage without six more copies of the same dynamic case. New §16 makes coverage a COMPUTED property, law 1's own mechanism: every `op === "<name>"` literal in a door's own source, read by regex, asserted against a hand-maintained `OP_COVERAGE` table so a new op fails the gate the day it ships without an entry - deliberately scoped to seven doors (the six named above plus `apply.js`, named for its WS-R48 `intent` widening) rather than all `EXPECTED_DOORS`, because building it even for just these seven surfaced 27 pre-existing, genuinely uncased owner-bearer ops (`decisions.md#ws-r44-computed-op-list-scoped-to-six-named-doors`), named `preexisting-uncased` in the coverage table rather than hidden behind a passing gate - a real, honest finding, not a vulnerability confirmed. Three GET-only doors that joined `api/` after WS-R38 (`room-embed.js`, `creators.js`, `sitemap.js`) are excluded from the door list outright on the same rule that already excludes `room-cohorts.js`: none reads a request body, consumes a session, or accepts a bearer, so none of the battery's eight attack classes apply (`decisions.md#ws-r44-get-doors-do-not-belong-in-the-door-list`). Zero findings required a fix in a door or a decision module: every new case passed against the real, unmodified code once this workstream's own new test/fixture bugs were fixed (a `threw()`-wrapped success value mistaken for a thrown error, twice, and four call sites needing fixture SQL ported from `evals/payouts/run.mjs`/`evals/creator-directory/run.mjs` rather than re-derived - `rejected.md#ws-r44-threw-helper-swallows-a-success-value`, `rejected.md#ws-r44-new-payout-and-directory-cases-needed-fixture-sql-this-workstream-had-not-yet-added`). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **19/19 on the untouched tree** (confirmed via a saved `git diff` patch, `git checkout -- .`, the gate, then `git apply`, never `git stash`) **and 19/19 after** every commit. `node evals/room-doors/run.mjs` alone: 109 to 297 assertions (+188), 15 doors unchanged, 0 failures both before and after, ~1.26s to ~1.32s standalone (1208ms to 1441ms inside the gate) - comfortably under the 3s ceiling (`measurements.md#ws-r44-door-battery-case-counts-2026-09-04`, superseding `ws-r38-door-battery-case-counts-2026-09-04` with a `supersedes` edge). `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls, unchanged. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1133 nodes / 1381 edges. No migration, no new env var, no network; two commits (`b816103` fixture SQL, `49a8995` the cases and the computed-list mechanism) on branch `ws-r44-door-battery-ops`, not pushed. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: nothing here touched a live database; the 27 `preexisting-uncased` ops and every op on the five doors this workstream's `OP_COVERAGE` mechanism does not cover (`checkins.js`, `handoff.js`, `pulse.js`, `replica.js`, `account.js`) remain genuinely unattacked by this battery, named rather than hidden.

Two real defects were hit and fixed before the gate passed clean, both logged in `rejected.md` because both are worth a future agent not re-discovering: a markdown-backtick-quoted comment inside `api/_replica-full-erasure.js`'s one giant JS template literal broke the module's own JS syntax — the FIFTH recorded instance of an already-four-times-documented defect in this exact file (`rejected.md#ws-r40-backtick-in-a-sql-comment-hit-a-fifth-time-in-the-same-file-despite-four-prior-writeups`, which also names the un-taken structural fix: a `node --check` gate over touched `api/*.js` files); and a double-quoted `tableApplied("vy_room_arrival")` call sitting between two unrelated backtick-quoted JSDoc mentions fooled `evals/room-leak/run.mjs`'s own naive backtick-pairing scanner into a silent, plausible-looking `no-statement-found` failure for a correct real query (`rejected.md#ws-r40-double-quoted-table-name-fooled-room-leaks-own-backtick-pairing-scanner`), fixed by naming the string as a constant declared away from any backtick. `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **19/19 before any edit and 19/19 after** (`measurements.md#ws-r40-gate-before-after-2026-09-04`), including `typecheck` clean, `chrome copy` (6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls) unchanged, `layout readability` unchanged with the new Share control present in both `room`/`room-hi` targets, and the full `eval suite` gate (every registered suite, including the new `room-share` one) exiting 0. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1137 nodes / 1389 edges.
