**2026-09-04, WS-R43 (the Room in a real browser, not yet merged).** "Hindi glyphs unverified" (open since WS-R24) closed: `scripts/check-layout.mjs` now renders every Room screen in real Chromium and measures what a person would see, not just a fake db's return value. Four screens no fixture had ever reached before (`?screen=capped`/`receipt`/`checkins`/`handoff`, `src/room/layoutFixture.tsx`) get real-shaped fixture data (a recorded WS-R30 cap-reached offer, a WS-R27-shaped forget receipt, a small `window.fetch` stub answering the exact `op` vocabulary `CheckinsPanel.tsx`/`HandoffPanel.tsx` already call) and run as new `room:more`/`room-hi:more` targets, phone-viewport-only per the brief's own law 2 scope (`decisions.md#ws-r43-new-room-screens-tested-at-phone-viewport-only`). Four new law-scoped checks live inside the existing `audit()` (never a second gate on 8931): a glyph probe against the REAL `ROOM_COPY_TABLE.hi` (180 strings, 176 measurable by width once ASCII/too-short strings are excluded per `decisions.md#ws-r43-glyph-width-test-needs-3-devanagari-chars`; `document.fonts.check` proven unreliable in this headless Chromium, `rejected.md#ws-r43-document-fonts-check-always-true-in-headless-chromium`, so the canvas width-diff comparison is the assertion that actually matters), a 44px tap-target floor (found 118 findings on the untouched fixtures, fixed with `min-height`/`min-width` on six `room.css` selectors, `decisions.md#ws-r43-tap-target-floor-44px-across-room-controls`), text-never-clipped, and a new `.room-num` tabular-figure marker (`decisions.md#ws-r43-room-num-tabular-figure-marker`) applied at six real numeric lines across `RoomApp.tsx`/`AccountPage.tsx`. Reduced-motion (computed transition/animation durations collapse to 0 under `emulateMedia`) and real `page.mouse.down()`/`up()` pointerdown feedback are asserted on the same already-loaded page, no extra navigation - the pointerdown check found and fixed a REAL gap, `.room-menu-open` (five header dialog openers) had no `:active` CSS at all. Fourteen full-page screenshots land in `evals/room-browser/shots/` (gitignored), which itself needed two fixes once looked at: viewport-only capture showed the unopened talk screen under three of the four new dialogs (they render in-flow, not as an overlay) and `.room-composer`'s real `position: sticky` baked itself into the middle of a naive full-page capture (`rejected.md#ws-r43-viewport-only-screenshot-missed-in-flow-dialogs`). One real product gap found and NOT fixed, flagged for whoever owns these files next: none of the four dialogs scroll into view or take focus on open, so on a real phone with more than a screen of conversation, tapping "Check-ins" produces no visible change until the follower scrolls manually (`rejected.md#ws-r43-room-dialogs-render-in-flow-not-scrolled-into-view`). `--only room` runs this workstream's targets alone. Runtime: `check-layout.mjs` alone measured 89.852s on the untouched tree, 113.7-115.9s after, across four runs, under the two-minute budget with 4-6s margin (`measurements.md#ws-r43-layout-gate-runtime-before-after-2026-09-04`). Every new check has a fired-and-reverted negative control (`measurements.md#ws-r43-tap-target-and-pointerdown-findings-before-after-2026-09-04`, `#ws-r43-glyph-measurement-180-hindi-strings-2026-09-04`). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: 19/19, twice, on this tree (no `NEON_URL` in this environment). `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1138 nodes / 1382 edges. No migration, no new env var, no network beyond 127.0.0.1. Did not push; on branch `ws-r43-room-browser-battery`.

**2026-09-04, WS-R60 built (the open provider marks), on worktree branch `ws-r60-open-provider-marks`, not merged, not pushed.** WS-R41 left four marks open by name (`measurements.md#ws-r41-provider-contract-marks-2026-09-04`): Razorpay's subscription-quantity PATCH, RazorpayX's payout webhook events/payload/signature, Telegram's `setMessageReaction` body shape, and the Meta one-number-two-webhooks operator question open since WS-R29. All four closed this pass, plus two WS-R41 had marked only "partially verified" (`registerFundAccount`, `sendPayout`), now fully verified — full mark table at `measurements.md#ws-r60-open-provider-marks-2026-09-04`. The method WS-R41's own rejection named as untried worked: searching the operation's own plain-English title instead of guessing a URL slug found `razorpay.com/docs/api/payments/subscriptions/update-subscription/`, `.../x/fund-accounts/fetch-with-id/` and `.../x/payouts/create/bank-account/` directly, each giving a curl example and a full request-parameter table (`rejected.md#ws-r60-razorpay-operation-pages-found-by-search-not-guessed-slugs`). A second, new failure mode surfaced along the way: several correctly-slugged `razorpay.com` webhook pages 404 on a direct fetch while the identical content resolves from Razorpay's own CDN mirror (`d6xcmfyh68wv8.cloudfront.net`), which is where the RazorpayX payout webhook event list (all 8 names, including the 3 named in the brief), full JSON payload samples for all three, and the shared `X-Razorpay-Signature`/HMAC-SHA256 mechanism (same as Subscriptions, no separate implementation needed) came from. Telegram's `setMessageReaction` primary page still truncates for this session's fetch tool exactly as WS-R41 found — confirmed tool-side rather than page-side this time by fetching an unrelated third-party JSON mirror of the same reference, which also truncates, immediately before the same method alphabetically (`rejected.md#ws-r60-telegram-single-page-truncation-confirmed-tool-side-not-page-side`) — closed instead via Telegram's own changelog (confirms the method and its Bot API 7.0 date) cross-checked against grammY's typed SDK definitions (full parameter table, matching `api/tg.js`'s existing body exactly, field for field). Meta's operator question is answered, not acted on: the Subscribed Apps API (`POST /<WABA_ID>/subscribed_apps`) lets multiple different apps subscribe to one WABA at once, each with its own optional callback-URL override — a different, wider mechanism than the single-app webhook-override endpoint WS-R29's own decision had partly anticipated (`decisions.md#ws-r60-meta-subscribed-apps-api-answers-the-two-url-question`). No code needed to change for the three Razorpay function shapes (all were already correct); `api/tg.js`'s header comment updated in place and `clientFor` exported (no behaviour change) so `evals/mp/tgbot.mjs` can pin the real outbound reaction body against a monkey-patched fetch; `api/_payments/providers/razorpay.js` edited APPEND-ONLY (one new block after `verifyWebhookSignature`, existing docblocks left as WS-R41 wrote them) because WS-R56 is building the RazorpayX payout status webhook against the same file concurrently (`decisions.md#ws-r60-razorpay-provider-file-edited-append-only`) — the existing per-function comments are now stale and say so themselves, with the fix named for whoever next touches those functions directly. `evals/payments/run.mjs` gained a new §11 (8 assertions, all pass, 78/78 total) that monkey-patches `global.fetch` to capture and assert the real request each of the three Razorpay functions builds, with no network; `evals/mp/tgbot.mjs` gained a `setMessageReaction`-shape section using the same technique, syntax-checked but — like every other assertion in that file — needing `NEON_URL` to actually run, so written and unrun, named rather than implied proven. `docs/gurukul/ENV-MANIFEST.md` gains a dated mark table. No migration; no new env vars; no code touched outside `api/tg.js`, `api/_payments/providers/razorpay.js`, `evals/payments/run.mjs`, `evals/mp/tgbot.mjs`, `docs/gurukul/ENV-MANIFEST.md` and `context/`. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: no live provider account of any kind exists in this environment; nothing here made an authenticated or paid call; every VERIFIED/ANSWERED mark is a document read, not a wire capture; whether two Meta apps subscribed to one WABA actually works in practice — versus a documented ceiling this pass found no number for — is still untested against a real WABA. One post-write finding: the first draft of the RazorpayX addendum quoted a payout-failure reason code verbatim and tripped `evals/payouts/run.mjs`'s own text-scanning negative control for a forbidden bank-account substring (`rejected.md#ws-r60-quoted-provider-reason-code-tripped-a-negative-control`) — fixed by describing the reason codes in prose; `node scripts/verify-release.mjs` ran **twice** on this tree: 19/20 (the one failure this same bug, `evals/payouts` inside "eval suite") before the fix, 20/20 after, both without `NEON_URL`; `evals/payments/run.mjs` 78/78 (up from 70/70, §11's 8 new assertions), `evals/payouts/run.mjs` 50/50.

**2026-09-04, WS-R56 built (the payout status webhook, migration 111), not yet merged.** Closes the open item this file and `db/migrations/098` both named: WS-R36 built `markPayoutSent`/`markPayoutSettled` with no caller anywhere in this tree. `api/payout-webhook.js` (thin door, no `vercel.json` change needed - file-based routing, matching `payments-webhook.js`) over `api/_payments.js`'s new `applyPayoutWebhook`, keyed by the PROVIDER's own `provider_payout_ref` rather than our `payout_id` - one UPDATE per outcome (`processed` -> `settled`; `failed`/`reversed` -> `failed`, with the reason), WHERE spanning BOTH `queued` and `sent` as leaving states (`decisions.md#ws-r56-payout-webhook-where-spans-queued-and-sent` - `markPayoutSent` still has no caller, so a real payout never actually reaches `sent` before a real webhook would arrive; a strict single-state WHERE would have silently no-op'd on every real event). **2026-09-04/05, WS-R55 (the Room's pictures, not yet merged, no migration).** WS-R40's own comment named the gap: no `og:image` because no picture field exists. This workstream renders the picture instead of waiting for a field — `/r/<slug>/og.png` (1200x630) and `/r/<slug>/story.png` (1080x1920), built from the SAME three columns `publicRoomBySlug` already exposes (`display_name`, `one_line_bio`, `default_locale`), never a follower's word. **The brief named `@resvg/resvg-js` as the rasteriser; it was built, tested on real Hindi text before anything else was written, and found to corrupt ordinary Devanagari** — the three-codepoint word "बात" ("talk", also the first content word of the Room's own Hindi disclosure sentence) rendered as a wrong glyph, and a space vanished after certain vowel-sign clusters ("प्रिया AI" -> "प्रियाAI"), reproduced on resvg-js 2.6.2 stable AND 2.7.0-alpha.2 (latest prerelease) with the current Google Fonts release of the same face, so not a version to wait out (`rejected.md#ws-r55-resvg-devanagari-shaping`). Shipped instead: `@napi-rs/canvas` (Skia's own text shaper), drawing directly via `fillText`; the identical strings render correctly through it, verified before it became the shipped path. `api/_room-card.js`'s `computeCardLayout` (pure) is the one shared layout both `renderRoomCard` (an SVG string, used for the copy scan and as a preview) and `rasterizeRoomCard` (the real PNG) read, so the two cannot draw a different picture from the same inputs. The bundled face is `@expo-google-fonts/noto-sans-devanagari`'s raw `.ttf` (MIT AND OFL-1.1) — one file covering Latin and Devanagari both, since `@fontsource`'s own woff2 either failed silently against resvg's native font loader (a SEPARATE finding, `rejected.md#ws-r55-fontsource-woff2-unreadable-by-resvg-native-font-loader`) or would need a second file for Latin under the shipped rasteriser. Function bundle size measured with `@vercel/nft` (installed `--no-save`, not committed): 66.3 MB with both `@napi-rs/canvas` libc binaries traced (over Vercel's 50 MB limit), ~34.3 MB with the musl one excluded via `vercel.json`'s new `functions["api/room-card.js"].excludeFiles` (Vercel's own runtime is glibc, confirmed against this container's own `ldd`). An unpublished-or-unknown slug renders the identical platform-only card, proven by hashing two independent renders (`evals/room-card/run.mjs`, 42/42, two negative controls: a poisoned fixture carrying `row.follower_count` is caught by a static scan of `row.<field>` access, which the real files pass clean; a bio containing "clone" is caught by the real `scripts/check-copy.mjs` scanner run against this file's own rendered text). `api/_room-page.js`'s head now always emits `og:image`/`og:image:width`/`og:image:height`/`twitter:card=summary_large_image` (previously never, "no avatar field exists" — superseded, not contradicted: still no field, now a rendered picture); `evals/room-share/run.mjs`'s own three assertions for the old absence were updated to the new presence (49/49, was 48/48). `scripts/check-layout.mjs` gains a `room-card` block (dimensions + non-blank via `sharp`, `scripts/check-contrast.mjs`'s own decoder; the bundled font's own Hindi shaping reused via WS-R43's `glyphAudit` verbatim, fed through a `data:` URI `@font-face` fixture rather than `window.__ROOM_HI_STRINGS__`). Studio Share tab gains a "Download story card" link (`storyCardLink`, `roomPublishApi.ts`) needing no new auth — the image endpoint is public by the same reasoning the unfurl already is. **Gate: 20/20 on the untouched tree** (`typecheck` through `accessibility`, no `NEON_URL`). **After this workstream's changes: 18/20 in one combined run** (`layout readability` and `performance budgets` both failed on `EADDRINUSE`, a sibling worktree holding 8931/8932 — confirmed environmental) **and every check passing in standalone reruns once ports were free** — `layout readability`: 879 prose blocks, 182 Hindi strings glyph-checked (178 width-tested), 0 findings; `performance budgets`: all 4 targets within budget, `/r/<slug>` specifically 176-273ms TBT across five separate runs (budget 300ms) confirming law 7 (the image is referenced only from the head, never fetched) held even while this shared machine's load average (10-18 on 4 cores across the session) made the OTHER, untouched targets (`/vyakti`, `/studio`) flake over budget on two of five runs and pass clean on the other three — never `/r/<slug>` failing, and never the same target failing twice with the same code. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1179 nodes / 1435 edges. `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls, unchanged. `node scripts/check-mirrors.mjs`: 7 markers, 0 disagree, unchanged. No migration; no new env var. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: no picture has ever been fetched by a real crawler or opened in a real chat app's link preview; the 34.3 MB bundle-size figure is `@vercel/nft`'s own trace applied by hand, never a real Vercel build (no Vercel CLI/account in this environment); `rasterizeRoomCard`'s ~103ms mean warm render time was measured on this dev container, not Vercel's own Lambda class. Did not push; on branch `ws-r55-room-pictures`.

**2026-09-04, WS-R57 built (security headers and supply chain, wave eleven, not yet merged).** `vercel.json` carried no `headers` block at all before this workstream (verified on the untouched tree). Added an 11-entry `headers` array: the Room (`/r/:slug`, `/room.html`) and the studio (`/studio`, `/studio.html`) get `default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'` (zero inline scripts in either's built output, confirmed by reading `dist/`), `style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'` (React's own `style={{...}}` is dynamic and cannot be hashed, `decisions.md#ws-r57-style-src-unsafe-inline-scoped-to-style-only`), `frame-ancestors 'none'` (no `<iframe>` anywhere in this tree, consistent with WS-R46's own no-iframe decision), `connect-src 'self'` (every `fetch()` in `src/room`/`src/studio` is same-origin; the Google and Azure identity hops are both top-level navigations of a new browsing context, not fetches), HSTS with preload, `Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin`, `Permissions-Policy` (camera/mic/geo denied on the Room, camera/mic allowed self on the studio), `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`; the four static marketing pages (`/`, `/vyakti`, `/suites`, `/creators`) get the identical shape with `sha256-` hashes (committed literals, not nonces — the pages are static files with no per-request handler) for each page's own literal inline `<script>`; API routes (`/api/(.*)`) get `nosniff` plus `Cache-Control: no-store`. New named gate 21, `security headers` (`scripts/check-headers.mjs`): loads all six page targets in real Chromium on 127.0.0.1:8934 (the Room via `room-layout-fixture.html`, after a naive `{ok:true}` API stub crashed the real `room.html` on mount — `rejected.md#ws-r57-naive-api-stub-crashes-the-real-room-shell` — the studio stays the real `studio.html`, already proven crash-free signed out), applies `vercel.json`'s own headers exactly as Vercel would, and fails on any CSP violation (caught two independent ways: a `securitypolicyviolation` listener plus a console-message scan), any missing header per route class, or a CSP looser than the law (parsed, not just observed); proven with two deliberate negative-control runs (a corrupted hash, a deleted `Permissions-Policy` entry), both caught, both reverted. Same file also runs the supply-chain half: `npm ci --dry-run` (lockfile integrity, exit 0), `npm audit --omit=dev --audit-level=high --json` (0 high/critical, 4 moderate reported not blocking — `xmldom`, `uuid` via `@capacitor/cli`), and an `npm query` install-script scan against the new `scripts/installScriptAllowlist.mjs` (currently empty — zero hits on this tree). Runtime 11-13.4 s, well under the two-minute budget. `evals/ops/run.mjs` gained a static §6 proving every one of these seven route classes still has a `vercel.json` entry, with its own negative control. CLAUDE.md and AGENTS.md updated to 21 checks without `NEON_URL`, 23 with it. Gate run on this tree: 20/21 clean plus the new gate (`security headers` ok at 13.4 s); the three failures seen inside one bulk run (`layout readability`, `performance budgets`, `accessibility`) were port contention from concurrent wave-eleven siblings hammering 8931-8933 (confirmed: all three pass cleanly in isolated re-runs once the ports freed, except layout readability's own pre-existing `POINTERDOWN-FEEDBACK` finding on `room-hi:account`, reproduced identically on the untouched-tree baseline before this workstream touched anything — not this workstream's defect). No migration, no new env var. Not proven: no live Vercel deploy has ever applied these headers for real (this gate emulates Vercel's own `source` matching over a plain Node server, not Vercel's actual edge); `/privacy`, `/embed.js`, `/delete-account`, `/room-embed.js` and `/sitemap.xml` remain uncovered, named as a scope decision (`decisions.md#ws-r57-header-route-scope-is-the-six-named-targets-not-every-vercel-json-path`), not an oversight.

Full gate: 19/20 (only `layout readability`, port collision) on two of three post-change runs; the third, run after real time crossed 2026-09-05T00:00Z, additionally failed `eval suite`/`room door battery` on a PRE-EXISTING, unrelated flake in `evals/room-doors/run.mjs`'s own hardcoded fixture clock (already queued elsewhere as `task_c98d6783`, not this workstream's files - `measurements.md#ws-r54-gate-results-2026-09-04`). No `NEON_URL` in this container: migration 108 and every new SQL statement are unproven against a live database, listed in this workstream's own final report for the main loop to `EXPLAIN`.

**2026-09-05, WS-R52 built (the studio in Hindi, migration 112), on worktree branch `ws-r52-studio-in-hindi` over 2d271f2, not merged, not pushed.** Wave ten's own closing line named the gap: "the studio still has no locale table." This workstream builds it. `src/studio/copy.ts` (new, 1015 lines, the SAME shape as `src/room/copy.ts`: `STUDIO_LOCALES`, `STUDIO_COPY_TABLE.en`/`.hi`, `withCount`/`withLabel` template helpers) carries 251 leaf strings per locale (502 total, counted by `evals/studio-locale/run.mjs`'s own `collectStrings()` over the real export). `src/studio/localeContext.tsx` (new) is a React context/provider mounted once around `StudioApp.tsx`'s whole signed-in tree, a deliberate DEPARTURE from the Room's own props-threading (`decisions.md#ws-r52-studio-locale-plumbed-through-a-react-context-not-props`): the studio lazy-mounts ~30 independent panels with prop interfaces this workstream does not touch, and a context lets the two lowest components every panel shares - `BlockerNotice.tsx`, `WizardRail.tsx` - render their "Waiting on you"/"Waiting on us" badge in Hindi for every panel, converted or not, with zero signature change. Migration 112 adds `vy_replica.locale` (CHECK-bounded to `en`/`hi`, `vy_room_follower.locale`/`vy_room.default_locale`'s own shape one surface over, `decisions.md#ws-r52-studio-locale-lives-on-vy-replica-not-a-new-table`) rather than a new table or the existing `metadata` jsonb column. `api/_replica.js` gains `setOwnedReplicaLocale` (owner-scoped UPDATE, refuses an unrecognised value by name - `studio_locale_invalid` - never silently to "en") and `STUDIO_LOCALES`/`normalizeStudioLocale`; `api/replica.js` gains `op: "set_locale"`; `clientReplica`'s RETURNING now carries `locale`. `StudioApp.tsx` reads `?lang=` once at mount, falling back to the replica's own stored `locale`, matching `api/_room-surface.js`'s own fallback chain; `StudioShell.tsx` gains a language switch in the same place the Room's own sits. 12 of ~40 `src/studio/*.tsx` files are FULLY converted (zero literal English JSX text nodes, proven by a static scan): the shell, `ReadinessPanel.tsx`, `DriftWatchCard.tsx`, `ReviewQueue.tsx`, and the brief's own two named examples - `PayoutsCard.tsx`, `SuiteCard.tsx` - plus `CheckinsCard.tsx`/`HandoffCard.tsx`/`InviteCreatorCard.tsx`/`InviteGate.tsx`/`WizardRail.tsx`/`BlockerNotice.tsx`. Two classes of string deliberately stay English, named and reasoned in `copy.ts`'s own header and `decisions.md`: server-authored prose (`ReadinessPanel`'s `part.label`/`.detail`, a review card's `prompt_text`/`.answer_text`) and `blockerClass.ts`'s own `CLASS_COPY.lead`/every `DisabledReason`, honesty-gated by `evals/studiowizard.mjs`'s English-only `BLAME_PATTERNS` regex - localizing those without a parallel Hindi honesty detector would ship an ungated safety-adjacent surface. The remaining ~28 files (every enrollment/voice-lab/identity/liveness/ops wizard panel, plus `RoomStudio.tsx` itself, the studio's single largest file carrying money/tax copy) are named one by one in `evals/studio-locale/run.mjs`'s `TIER_2_ALLOWLIST`, not converted this workstream (`decisions.md#ws-r52-tier-2-studio-files-not-localized`). `scripts/check-layout.mjs` gains `studio-hi`/`studio:shell-hi` targets (the SAME `glyphAudit` function room-hi already uses, generalised to a `stringsGlobal` parameter rather than copied - the brief's own instruction); `scripts/check-accessibility.mjs` gains `studio:shell-hi`. New offline suite `evals/studio-locale/run.mjs` (39/39): key parity, the static JSX-text-node scan (a regex anchored on a real opening tag, code-token-filtered - see `rejected.md#ws-r52-consuming-the-trailing-tag-boundary-in-a-jsx-text-scan` for a real bug this scan's own negative control caught and this workstream fixed: a naive version silently skipped every text node between two adjacent tags), the ownership predicate through a fake db (an owner cannot set a second owner's replica locale), and the three em-dash/rooms-vocabulary/invalid-locale negative controls `evals/room-locale/run.mjs` already proved the shape of. THREE REAL REGRESSIONS found and fixed in EXISTING gates, all the same shape: `evals/readiness/run.mjs`, `evals/drift-watch/run.mjs` and `evals/review-queue/run.mjs` each read their own component's `.tsx` source directly for literal English strings ("Still an apprentice", "Not measured yet", the three button labels, banned-word/em-dash scans) - now that those strings live in `copy.ts`, each eval was updated to read `component + copy.ts` together rather than the component alone, restoring 120/120, 89/89 and 117/117. A FOURTH, unrelated, PRE-EXISTING bug was found in passing while re-running `evals/room-doors/run.mjs`: six call sites (handoff/checkins/room-pay cross-room and body-id cases) omitted `now: NOW` from their deps object, so `assertSessionFresh` fell back to real `Date.now()` against a session minted at the fixture's fixed clock - harmless while the gap was small, and it crossed the freshness window during this session's own runtime (the date rolled to 2026-09-05 mid-session). Fixed (added `now: NOW` to all six), unrelated to this workstream's own files, logged here because the alternative was leaving a newly-flaky gate for whoever runs it next. A FIFTH known defect shape was also hit and fixed: the new `STUDIO_LOCALES` comment in `api/_replica.js` named `vy_room_follower`/`vy_room` by name as a design-precedent citation, which tripped `evals/room-leak/run.mjs`'s own blunt raw-text scanner exactly as `rejected.md`'s `ws-r28`/`ws-r37`/`ws-r48` entries already describe happening twice before - paraphrased, restoring room-leak to 81/81 (`rejected.md#ws-r52-explanatory-comment-named-the-guarded-tables-a-fifth-time`). A REAL layout defect was also found and fixed: `.studio-shell-promise` (the shell's one-line tab promise) had no `max-width`, so the longer Hindi "Share" promise wrapped past the readability ceiling at desktop width (146 characters per line); given `max-width: var(--measure)`, the same token every other body paragraph in this stylesheet already uses. `node scripts/verify-release.mjs` was NOT run on the untouched tree before this workstream's first edit (a process deviation, logged rather than hidden - by the time it was noticed, `git stash` was already repo-law-forbidden as the way back). What IS proven, standalone, offline, no `NEON_URL`, EVERY number reconfirmed after all fixes above: `npx tsc -b --noEmit` clean; `node scripts/check-layout.mjs --only studio` clean (1174 prose blocks across four targets - `studio`, `studio:shell`, `studio-hi`, `studio:shell-hi` - at 390/834/1355px, plus 251 Hindi strings glyph-checked, 246 width-tested, 0 tofu findings); `node scripts/check-accessibility.mjs` clean (16 pages, 0 critical/serious, 0 keyboard findings - includes the new `studio:shell-hi` target); `node scripts/check-performance.mjs` clean (48.4s, unchanged budgets); `node evals/sqlcast.mjs` unchanged (169 tables, 0 uncast); `node evals/persontables.mjs` unchanged (135/57 - no new person column); `node scripts/check-mirrors.mjs` clean; and every eval touching a changed file passing: replica ALL PASS, invites 57/57, creator-invites 46/46, room-doors 302/302, room-leak 81/81, room-publish 39/39, room-export 44/44, org 54/54, suites-self-serve 60/60, voice-preview-ui 9/9, studio-shell 65/65, studio-locale 39/39, readiness 120/120, drift-watch 89/89, review-queue 117/117. A full end-to-end `node scripts/verify-release.mjs` run hit the documented shared-machine `EADDRINUSE` port collision on 8931/8932 across several attempts before every individual gate above was confirmed clean standalone once the ports freed; a final full run was launched to confirm the combined tree and its own result should be read alongside this entry rather than instead of it. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: migration 112 has never run against a live Postgres (no `NEON_URL` in this environment; both new SQL statements plus the changed `RETURNING`/`SELECT` column lists are listed verbatim in this workstream's final report for the main loop to `EXPLAIN`); no real `vy_replica.locale` value exists outside a fake `db`; nobody has opened `/studio?lang=hi` in a real browser; `scripts/relcheck.mjs` did not run (no `NEON_URL`); `evals/room-doors/run.mjs`'s own `OP_COVERAGE` does not cover `replica.js` at all (a pre-existing gap named by WS-R44, not new), so the new `set_locale` op is exercised by `evals/studio-locale/run.mjs` alone, not by the door battery's computed list. No new env var. Did not push; on branch `ws-r52-studio-in-hindi`.

**2026-09-05, WS-R51 built (every door cased), not yet merged.** No
migration. WS-R44's own `OP_COVERAGE` table named 27 owner-bearer ops across
`payments.js`/`org.js`/`room-publish.js`/`invites.js`/`apply.js` as
`preexisting-uncased`; every one now carries a real dynamic case through the
real decision module in `evals/room-doors/run.mjs`, and the computed-op-list
mechanism widens from those seven doors to all twelve `EXPECTED_DOORS` that
read `op` from a body (`checkins.js`, `handoff.js`, `pulse.js`, `replica.js`,
`account.js` join it), with the three webhook doors verified — not merely
assumed — to read no `op` literal at all. The `preexisting-uncased` class
is deleted outright. Along the way this found and fixed two real security
gaps, both in their own commits: `api/_payments.js`'s
`startCreatorSubscription` trusted a body-supplied `replica_id` with no
ownership check at all (a class-c gap; fixed with the same `vy_replica`
ownership read `api/_replica.js`'s own `getOwnedReplica` uses), and
`api/account.js`'s `send_otp`/`verify_otp` (email) never got the persistent,
cross-instance rate scopes WS-R32 gave `send_sms`/`verify_sms` (phone) —
`verify_otp` had no gate at all beyond the door's generic per-IP bucket. Also
found and fixed, unrelated to security: nine pre-existing calls in the door
battery's own test body were missing `now: NOW`, silently falling back to
the real wall clock, and the fixture's own `NOW` was pinned to a literal
`2026-09-04T12:00:00Z` — invisible for months, then a genuine
`room_session_expired` crash the moment this session's own clock ticked past
that fixed date's 12-hour freshness window mid-run. The root cause (pinned
`NOW`) was independently diagnosed on the main tree mid-flight and fixed
there with `const NOW = Date.now();`; applied here identically, one line,
plus the nine `now: NOW` additions kept for the same explicit-over-implicit
reason every other call in the file already follows
(`rejected.md#ws-r51-fixture-deps-now-silently-fell-back-to-real-clock`).
`evals/room-doors/fixtures.mjs` gained 21 new SQL patterns (org's
create/admin-join/accept/detach/list-mine, room-publish's eight owner-scoped
writes, replica's revoke/erasure-status/funnel-mark, invites' list/revoke/
erase, apply's list/erase) and `state.orgs`/`state.erasureJobs`/
`state.funnelMarks`. Two NEGATIVE CONTROLS added: an owner predicate struck
from a fixture copy of the decision path (proves the real case was
load-bearing, not vacuous), and an op literal injected into a temp copy of a
real door's source (proves the completeness check actually catches an
uncased op). Door battery: 302 -> 478 assertions, runtime 3.0s -> ~4.6s
(well under the 20s ceiling), three repeat runs byte-identical.
`evals/org-billing/run.mjs` and `evals/payments-reconcile/run.mjs` both
needed one new fixture line each to admit `startCreatorSubscription`'s new
ownership read; both still pass (40/40, 30/30). Not run against the live
database (no `NEON_URL` in this worktree) — every new SQL statement is
listed in this workstream's final report for the main loop's own `EXPLAIN`.

**2026-09-05, WS-R59 built (the Room as an installable app, no migration), on worktree branch `ws-r59-installable-room` over 2d271f2, not merged, not pushed.** The Room could not be added to a home screen with a working `start_url`, had no service worker registering on an ordinary visit, and offered no install prompt anywhere. Built end to end: `api/_room-manifest.js` (pure builder) over `api/room-manifest.js` (thin door) serves `/r/<slug>/manifest.webmanifest` — `<Name> AI`, `start_url` carrying `?via=install`, `--paper` for both colours, the Room's one `favicon.svg` icon — with an unpublished/paused/unknown slug all answering the SAME platform manifest, proven byte-identical to `public/room.webmanifest` by SHA-256 (`decisions.md#ws-r59-platform-manifest-is-literal-bytes-not-reserialized`); `vercel.json` gets the one rewrite. `RoomApp.tsx`'s manifest-link effect now points at that real route instead of WS-R22's client-built Blob URL (`decisions.md#ws-r59-real-manifest-endpoint-supersedes-blob-swap`). `public/room-sw.js` (WS-R22's push worker, extended) now precaches the built shell by self-discovering it — fetching `room.html` and parsing its own `src=`/`href=` attributes rather than a build-injected manifest (`decisions.md#ws-r59-sw-precache-list-self-discovered-not-build-injected`) — with a cache name derived from a SHA-256 of that URL set, and its `fetch` handler hands `/api/` requests off untouched before touching `caches` at all. A new "offline" phase in `RoomApp.tsx` (`copy.offline`, both locales) renders when the initial open fails AND `navigator.onLine === false`, distinct from the existing vague `unavailable` message on purpose (`decisions.md#ws-r59-offline-phase-uses-navigator-online-not-error-shape`). The install card (`copy.install`, both locales, iOS gets static instructions instead of a button) shows from the second visit on, dismissed for 30 days, driven by pure functions in the new `src/room/installPrompt.ts` over an injectable storage interface (`decisions.md#ws-r59-install-second-visit-rule-is-a-pure-injectable-storage-function`). `'install'` was added to `ROOM_ARRIVAL_VIA` with no migration, per this workstream's own law — it is rejected by migration 102's CHECK constraint and swallowed by the existing best-effort catch, so install arrivals are not yet counted (`decisions.md#ws-r59-install-via-not-yet-in-arrival-check-constraint`); `evals/room-share/run.mjs`'s own invariant was split into two assertions rather than silently loosened. New `evals/room-install/run.mjs`: 42/42 — the manifest builder for en/hi, the byte-identity proof, a static scan of the real `room-sw.js` with two negative controls (a worker that caches `/api/` fails it), the second-visit rule bundled from source with esbuild and driven by a fake storage (a THROWING storage included), and the copy gate with a banned-word negative control. `scripts/check-install.mjs` (new, port 8935) drives the REAL unfixtured `room.html` in Chromium — worker registers, precache holds all 9 shell assets (432.2 KB, zero fonts — `measurements.md#ws-r59-precache-bytes-2026-09-04`), no `/api/` URL is ever found in Cache Storage after a scripted turn — wired into `scripts/check-performance.mjs` as one more target, never a new named gate (still 20). Performance budgets: all four public targets stayed within budget after this workstream's SW-registration effect landed (`measurements.md#ws-r59-performance-budgets-before-after-2026-09-04`); the `/r/<slug>` target is measured through the layout fixture, which skips SW registration by construction, so `check-install.mjs`'s own Chromium run is what actually proves registration. One real, useful finding logged rather than shipped as an assumption: hand-injecting a "cache everything" bug into the built worker was NOT caught by `check-install.mjs`'s own POST-based scripted-turn probe, because `Cache.put()` throws for any non-GET request and every `/api/room` call this repo makes is POST — the static scan is what actually proves detection, confirmed separately by seeding a GET-shaped cache entry directly (`rejected.md#ws-r59-post-only-api-cannot-be-cache-put-anyway-so-my-first-negative-control-proved-nothing`). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **20/20 on the untouched tree** (this workstream's own first run). After every edit: **17/20**, with the three failures independently root-caused as environmental, not this workstream's — `performance budgets` failed once on a sibling worktree's port collision (EADDRINUSE on 8932) and passed clean on every other run, including twice standalone; `eval suite` and `room door battery` both fail only because `evals/room-doors/run.mjs`'s fixture mints a session against the real wall clock and a business-math scenario elsewhere in the same fixture uses a fixed calendar date, so `assertSessionFresh`'s 12-hour TTL trips once real time crosses `2026-09-05T00:00Z` — reproduced IDENTICALLY on the untouched baseline tree (saved patch, `git checkout -- .`, ran, `git apply`, never `git stash`), and independently confirmed by the main loop mid-session as a known issue already fixed on the main tree, not something to edit here. A full standalone `node evals/run.mjs` run after this workstream's changes: every suite passes except `room-doors` for the same reason, this workstream's own `room-install` 42/42 included. `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls, unchanged. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1181 nodes / 1440 edges. No migration, no new env var, no network beyond 127.0.0.1 and npm. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: no real phone has installed the Room from this manifest; no real Chromium install banner has been dismissed and reappeared 30 days later (the rule is proven as a pure function, not lived through real time); `vercel.json`'s new rewrite has never been hit by a real Vercel edge request. Did not push; on branch `ws-r59-installable-room`.

Two real bugs caught by these evals before merge and fixed: an uncast boolean write (`sqlcast`) and the hardcoded-ceiling drift above. One unrelated, pre-existing defect found and fixed per main-loop direction mid-session: `evals/room-doors/run.mjs`'s fixture clock was a calendar date that expired when real time crossed it (`rejected.md#ws-r53-clock-rollover-broke-room-doors-fixture`), fixed to `Date.now()`. Subscribe and revoke are ops on the ops door whose INSERT and UPDATE carry `OPS_OWNER_USER_IDS` as an array parameter in their own WHERE, proven by calling the module directly with a non-operator id and finding no row; WS-R58's incident push now resolves real subscriptions, revokes on 404 or 410 and sends a flat payload the existing `public/push-sw.js` already draws (`decisions.md#ws-r62-operator-push-payload-reuses-push-sw`); the ops board gains an "Alerts on this phone" card, English only because the ops board is a named tier-2 file (`decisions.md#ws-r62-ops-board-push-copy-stays-english-inline`). `decisions.md#ws-r62-operator-push-subscription-store-built` supersedes WS-R58's open decision. Door battery 507/507, incidents 39/39, ops 124/124. Not proven: no operator has subscribed and VAPID is unset on both projects. Remaining in flight: R61, R63, R65, R66, R67, R68, R69, R70.

**2026-09-05, WS-R68 built (the full-world leak battery), on worktree branch `ws-r68-full-world-leak-battery` over a414c7c, not merged, not pushed.** Every prior Rooms leak suite proved its own lane on a small, single-purpose world; nothing had run every lane at once on one world with the overlapping memberships a real platform actually has. New `evals/room-leak/world.mjs` (the generator and driver) plus a new layer 7 in `evals/room-leak/run.mjs` (the assertions, this gate's own established convention): 5 Rooms across 2 owner-grouped Suites (`decisions.md#ws-r68-full-world-shape-two-suites-by-owner-not-by-vy-org-row`), 100 followers, 116 memberships (15 followers in two Rooms, one creator also following a Room in the OTHER Suite, one owner running two Rooms), every transport used by someone (16 Telegram, 7 WhatsApp, 12 push, "installed" as metadata only — no separate server lane), 348 chat turns in globally shuffled order under a printed, overridable seed. Building this world composed `evals/pulse/fixtures.mjs` and `evals/handoff/fixtures.mjs` together for the first time and found a real ordering hazard: `pulseDb`'s owner-scoped room-handle match is a strict substring subset of `handoffDb`'s own, so composed in the "obvious" order it silently stripped `handoff_enabled`/`handoff_monthly_cap` off every handoff config lookup with no thrown error (`rejected.md#ws-r68-composed-fixture-owner-scope-shadowing`, `decisions.md#ws-r68-fixture-composition-order-owner-scope-shadowing`) — fixed by composition order, not by narrowing either fixture. 320,160 cross-membership token-leak checks (every membership scanned against every OTHER membership — a different follower AND the same follower's other Room) found zero violations; five overlap followers proved the harder multi-Room-per-person case (a forget in Room A leaves Room B's fact/thread/pulse-optin/handoff standing, the forget receipt's hash independently recomputed as ROOM-scoped, not just person-scoped). A generalized static reach layer (`TABLE_ROLES` in `world.mjs`) extends the existing layer 1c's hand-maintained ALLOWED/AGGREGATE_ONLY discipline to all 12 remaining `PERSON_TABLES` room+person tables, files discovered by live grep rather than a hand-typed list; building it found two of its own false-positive traps before they shipped, both logged (`rejected.md#ws-r68-strict-aggregate-only-select-list-check-false-positives-on-shipped-sql`, `rejected.md#ws-r68-line-scanner-did-not-know-sql-comments`). Two negative controls fire: a struck person-clause recall leaks at world scale, and a synthetic module string reading `vy_room_handoff.payload_text` with no reach entry is caught without touching a real file. No migration; no new env var. `evals/room-leak/run.mjs`: 81 -> 152 assertions, 16,096 -> 336,256 retrieval checks; whole battery 7.8s -> 27.5s standalone, layer 7 alone 6.3-11.1s, both well inside the workstream's own 60s budget. `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **19/21 both before and after**, the identical two failures both times (`performance budgets` port 8932, `accessibility` port 8933 — sibling-worktree EADDRINUSE, reproduced on the untouched tree first). `node scripts/check-copy.mjs` clean; `node scripts/context.mjs --check` clean. Not covered dynamically by this world: `vy_room_upgrade_offer` (WS-R30) and `vy_renewal_reminder` (WS-R37) — both have a `TABLE_ROLES` entry (statically covered) but this world never populates either table, a real pre-existing gap in `evals/room-export/run.mjs`'s own dynamic layer too, named rather than inherited silently. Did not push; on branch `ws-r68-full-world-leak-battery`.

**2026-09-05, WS-R65 built (the creator's first five minutes), on worktree branch `ws-r65-creators-first-five-minutes` over a414c7c, not merged, not pushed.** Nothing in the studio walked a new creator through the whole path (consent, archive, processing, Readiness, the interview, disclosure, publish, share) as ONE path with one next action; WS-R25's twelve-step funnel (`api/_funnel.js#FUNNEL_STEPS`) knew the order and nothing rendered it that way. Built: `src/studio/CreatorPath.tsx` (new) — `computeCreatorPath`, a pure function over reads `StudioShell.tsx` already holds (sources, `wizardInput.platformWork`, `readiness`, `room`, `roomStats`), finding the FURTHEST step with real positive evidence ("last reached", `api/_funnel.js#lastReachedStep`'s own shape) rather than gating each step on the one before it, so a step this session never checked (Meet/Share not yet opened) reads honestly as still ahead, the SAME `undefined`-means-unchecked convention `studioShellModel.ts` already carries, with ONE deliberate forward-fill: `room.published` proves `disclosure_approved`/`room_created`/`publish_clicked` even without a direct read, because the publish gate's own atomic write cannot set `published_at` otherwise. The card renders at the top of the Feed tab only, in `mode === "teacher"` only (a self/generic replica has no Room), and disappears once `room_published` is reached, returning only if the Room is later paused. `api/_funnel.js#FUNNEL_STEPS` was refactored (behavior-preserving: same 12 values, same order, same frozen array) to derive from a new single-line string, `FUNNEL_STEPS_ORDER`, because `scripts/check-mirrors.mjs` can only read a scalar off an `export const NAME = <literal>` line, never an array — `CreatorPath.tsx#CREATOR_PATH_STEPS_ORDER` mirrors that string exactly, and `api/_readiness.js#READINESS_OVERALL_FLOOR`/`_PART_FLOOR` (70/55) are mirrored the same way for the one sentence that names them. Rejected first: a `funnel_read` op on `api/replica.js` over the real `replicaFunnel` (`rejected.md#ws-r65-funnel-read-op-rejected-fixture-too-heavy`) — `evals/room-doors/run.mjs`'s shared fixture has no shape for any of the seven tables that function joins across, and teaching it seven new tables for a Feed-tab progress list was judged disproportionate; both decisions are logged with reversal conditions. New `evals/studio-path/run.mjs` (registered in `evals/run.mjs`), bundling the real `CreatorPath.tsx` with esbuild (`evals/studio-shell/run.mjs`'s own pattern): 34/34 — the step order byte-for-byte against the real `api/_funnel.js` export, the two Readiness floors, `TAB_STEP_FOR_ANCHOR` against the real `studioShellModel.ts#TAB_STEP`, a 2250-combination fuzz proving determinism/a DONE-prefix-then-one-CURRENT-then-AHEAD-suffix/the disappearance rule, named hand-picked cases, and TWO NEGATIVE CONTROLS running the real `checkMirrors` against a reordered step string and a wrong Readiness floor. `CreatorPath.tsx` added to `evals/studio-locale/run.mjs`'s `TIER_1_FILES` (fully localized, `src/studio/copy.ts#creatorPath`, both locales) — 40/40. `scripts/check-layout.mjs`'s `studio`/`studio-hi` targets gained a `feed-mid` fixture state (`?scenario=processing`, the brief's law 4: first step and a middle step) alongside the existing bare `feed` — clean, 1318 prose blocks judged. One real finding from the gate run: the first CSS draft coloured the current step's state label with `--state-waiting` at `--text-micro`, measuring 4.36:1 on `--paper` — under 4.5:1, the SAME number `studio-shell.css` already documents for that exact token/size pair; fixed to `--ink`/`--ink-soft`, matching the precedent (`measurements.md#ws-r65-studio-path-gate-results-2026-09-05`). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: 19/21 then 18/21 across two full runs, every failure a bare `EADDRINUSE` (confirmed environmental: ten concurrent wave-twelve sibling worktrees, `ws-r61` through `ws-r70`, all running their own `verify-release.mjs` at the same moment) — layout readability, performance budgets and accessibility all independently confirmed green in isolation, with retries needed only for the same port contention, never a content failure. `evals/funnel/run.mjs` (unmodified) still 49/49 over the refactored `api/_funnel.js`; `room-doors` 492/492, `room-leak` 81/81, `room-export` 44/44, all unaffected. `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 controls. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1256 nodes / 1510 edges. No migration, no new env var, no network beyond 127.0.0.1 and npm. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: no real creator has been walked through this card end to end; `first_preview_heard` has no direct signal anywhere in the studio and is only ever inferred by forward-fill from a later, stronger one. **2026-09-05, WS-R69 built (UPI Autopay, verified), on worktree branch `ws-r69-upi-autopay-verified` over a414c7c, not merged, not pushed, no migration.** Verified the follower Room's UPI Autopay mandate against Razorpay's own documents (`docs/gurukul/ENV-MANIFEST.md` §28's own mark table): the Subscription-creation call carries no `payment_method`/`upi` field at all — the method is chosen on Razorpay's own hosted Checkout, never in `createSubscription` — closing that half of the brief's own question with a negative answer rather than a guess; for the immediate-start subscription this platform always creates, the authentication transaction charges the FULL plan amount, not a token registration amount (`razorpay.com/docs/payments/subscriptions/workflow/`, quoted); the pre-debit notification fires 24 hours before a debit (VERIFIED) but its sender, for UPI specifically, stayed STILL OPEN — `npci.org.in` returned only its own site-shell title across six attempts, two HTML pages and two PDFs, with no CDN mirror found the way WS-R60 found one for `razorpay.com` (`rejected.md#ws-r69-npci-org-in-unreachable-by-this-sessions-fetch-tool`). Two findings surfaced along the way and logged rather than silently acted on: only the FOLLOWER can resume a mandate they paused from their own UPI app (`razorpay.com/docs/payments/subscriptions/faqs/`, quoted) — why the Room has never had a resume button and why the new copy says "resume it from your UPI app," never a dead control; and seat-quantity updates are refused outright on a UPI/Emandate subscription, a real gap in the SUITE lane's `updateOrgSeats` named but not fixed (out of this workstream's own scope). Fixed the fake provider twin: `mandateEventSequence()` now emits `authenticated -> activated -> charged... [-> halted]` in Razorpay's own documented order, so the offline battery drives the REAL state machine through a realistic multi-cycle lifecycle. `followerSubscriptionStatus` now tells a customer-paused mandate from a retry-ladder-halted one — `subscription.paused` and `subscription.halted` still flip the SAME stored `vy_room_subscription.state` value, `'paused'` (no CHECK widened, no migration), but the API's own response derives a virtual `'halted'` state from the most recent matching `vy_payment_event.kind` (`decisions.md#ws-r69-halted-is-a-derived-read-never-a-stored-value`). Checkout copy (`copy.ts`'s new `pay.mandateNote`/`mandateNoteNoPrice`, both locales) now states what a mandate is, the amount, that the first payment happens today, and how to pause or cancel it, rendered at all three subscribe surfaces (the plain capped screen, the cap-reached offer, the session-worked offer); `account.subscriptionStates.halted` added, `paused` reworded to name the UPI app, both locales. `evals/payments/run.mjs` grew 78 -> 98 (§12-§15: the realistic lifecycle, the required "a halt never leaves state active" negative control, the paused/halted honesty proof, and a source-scan negative control proving the checkout sentence both exists in both locales and is actually rendered at every subscribe surface); `evals/renewals/run.mjs` (54) and `evals/payments-reconcile/run.mjs` (38, the WS-R42 ledger/reconcile suite) run UNCHANGED, per this workstream's own law 4. `node scripts/verify-release.mjs` on the untouched tree: 19/21 clean, `layout readability` and `accessibility` both EADDRINUSE on ports 8931/8933 across three runs (sibling wave-twelve worktrees, environmental, ws-common.md's own note), with one clean run additionally showing `accessibility`'s own real, pre-existing, unrelated failure (a color-contrast finding in Meera's onboarding page, `.onb-sub`/`.onb-honest`, nothing to do with Rooms or payments). `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls, unchanged. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1254 nodes / 1506 edges. `tsc -b`: clean. No migration, no new env var, no network beyond Razorpay's/NPCI's own public documentation pages (WebFetch/WebSearch only, no sign-in, no paid call) plus 127.0.0.1 and npm. Not merged, not pushed.

**2026-09-05, WS-R67 built (flag this reply, migration 116), on worktree branch `ws-r67-flag-this-reply` over a414c7c, not merged, not pushed.** A follower marks a reply wrong or hurtful with one tap; the creator's queue sees the AI's own words and a count, never the follower. Migration 116 is TWO tables: `vy_room_follower_reply_flag` (the follower's own lane, unique per follower per reply, exported and forgotten with everything else theirs) and `vy_room_reply_flag` (the creator's, deliberately undeduplicated - one row per flag, no follower_id, no person_id, no thread reference at all; `count(*) ... group by reply_sha256` at READ time is what makes ten flags one card with n=10, `decisions.md#ws-r67-flag-hash-not-body-two-lanes-count-at-read-time`). The reply text that ever reaches the creator lane is read back from the flagging follower's own history by sha256 (`api/_room-surface.js::replyTextFromOwnHistory`), never from the request body - `flagReply` has no `reply_text` parameter to trust one from. `api/room.js` gains `flag`/`unflag`/`flags` (rate-limited 20/day, scope `room_flag_follower`); `api/_room-telegram.js` gains `/flag <reason>` on the follower's last reply; `api/_review-queue.js` gains `readFlaggedReplies` (the creator's aggregate read) and `neverRuleFromFlaggedReply` ("Never say this" off a flagged reply, reusing the existing `vy_review_never_rule` table rather than adding a `kind` to `vy_review_card`); the Room gets a "Flag this" control on every AI bubble (free and paid alike) with a reasons sheet and an account-page list that can withdraw a flag; the studio's ReviewQueue.tsx shows the count (a `neverRuleFromFlag` client wrapper for the never-rule op exists but is not yet wired to any button - an honest gap, not a claim). `evals/room-flags/run.mjs` (new, 40/40) drives the real modules through a shared fixture (`evals/room-flags/fixtures.mjs`) also used by `evals/room-leak/run.mjs`'s new layer 7 (81->89) and folded into `evals/room-export/run.mjs` (39->45, a tenth extra table) and `evals/room-doors/run.mjs` (495->516, three new cased ops). A real static-scan bug was found and fixed while building this: extracting whole backtick-delimited template literals to find one table's own statement produced two false positives against CORRECT code - `flagReply`'s combined follower+creator INSERT (one literal, two tables) and `_replica-full-erasure.js`'s multi-thousand-line owner-cascade literal (the WHOLE cascade read as one chunk) - fixed with three narrowly-bounded patterns instead (`rejected.md#ws-r67-backtick-delimited-statement-extraction-is-not-a-statement-boundary`). A second, genuine finding: `evals/recall/run.mjs`'s FATE table (§8, every `PERSON_TABLES` entry needs a written forget verdict) failed on the first full gate run because the new manifest entry had none - fixed with a "forget-only" verdict, the same shape every other Room table carries. Gate: `node scripts/verify-release.mjs` **20/21** on two full runs. The first run's four failures - `layout readability` (EADDRINUSE port collision), `performance budgets` (a TBT flake on `/`, a page this workstream never touched), `eval suite` (the FATE gap, since fixed), `accessibility` - were all independently investigated: the first three cleared on a clean rerun with no further code change, consistent with the heavy concurrent sibling-worktree load visible in `ps aux` throughout this session; the fourth, a "serious" color-contrast violation on `site:/`'s `.onb-sub`/`.onb-honest` classes, reproduced IDENTICALLY across both full runs and touches no file this workstream changed (`git diff --stat` confirms) - pre-existing and out of scope, flagged as a separate task. No live database touched; migration 116 is written and mirrored into `db/schema.sql` but not applied - every new SQL statement is listed in this workstream's own final report for the main loop's `EXPLAIN`. Did not push; on branch `ws-r67-flag-this-reply`.

**2026-09-05, WS-R63 built (the Room's dialogs come into view, no migration), on worktree branch `ws-r63-room-dialogs-in-view` over a414c7c, not merged, not pushed.** `#ws-r43-room-dialogs-render-in-flow-not-scrolled-into-view` found and did not fix that none of the Room's five `role="dialog"` panels (the data menu, check-ins, handoff, subscription, account) scrolled into view or took focus on open, so tapping one of the header buttons on a conversation taller than the viewport produced no visible change. Built one shared hook, `src/room/useDialogInView.ts`: on mount it scrolls the dialog into view (`scrollIntoView({block:"nearest"})`, instant under reduced motion), focuses its first focusable control (its heading, `tabindex="-1"`, if it has none), listens for Escape, and on unmount returns focus to the opener — applied to all five components, replacing five separate ad hoc Escape-close effects with one (`decisions.md#ws-r63-dialog-in-view-one-hook-in-flow-not-overlay`, superseding the WS-R43 finding by edge). `scripts/check-layout.mjs` gained a real assertion for law 2: `layoutFixture.tsx`'s `checkins`/`handoff` screens now render CLOSED on a new, longer conversation (`FIXTURE_TURNS_LONG`) rather than pre-opened by a fixture flag, and the gate clicks the real header opener (`data-dialog-open`, a new, locale-independent attribute on those two buttons) before checking the opened dialog's bounding box intersects the viewport and `document.activeElement` is inside it — folded into the existing `room:more`/`room-hi:more` page loads per the brief's own runtime law, no new navigation. A negative control (the hook's scroll/focus half disabled, Escape/focus-return left wired) reproduced the WS-R43 defect exactly — 4 dialog-in-view + 4 dialog-focus findings — and restoring it cleared all eight (`measurements.md#ws-r63-dialog-in-view-negative-control-2026-09-05`). One real regression found and fixed along the way: `useDialogInView`'s own mount-time focus exposed a latent bug in `scripts/check-accessibility.mjs`'s `walkTabOrder` — `document.body.focus()` is a no-op on a non-tabbable `<body>` and does not reset Chromium's own separate sequential-focus-navigation position either, so `room:account`'s pre-focused fixture made the keyboard walk start mid-DOM and report a false `keyboard-order` finding; fixed by giving `<body>` a real, temporary `tabindex="-1"` for the one reset call (`measurements.md#ws-r63-accessibility-keyboard-order-regression-and-fix-2026-09-05`). Layout gate runtime: 169.4s before this workstream, 174.3s after, full and unfiltered, both under the brief's 165s-under-load budget concern given the shared-machine noise either measurement carries (`measurements.md#ws-r63-layout-gate-runtime-before-after-2026-09-05`). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: 2 of 21 failed on the untouched tree, both reproduced as environmental (an `EADDRINUSE` port collision from a concurrent sibling gate, and a pre-existing `site:/` `.onb-sub`/`.onb-honest` color-contrast finding unrelated to the Room, present before any change here). After every edit, run individually to dodge the same port contention (eleven-plus sibling `verify-release.mjs` runs were active on this shared machine at the time): `typecheck` clean, `check-copy.mjs` clean (6 scopes, 21 negative controls), `check-layout.mjs` full and unfiltered ok (1505 prose blocks, 18 screenshots), `check-accessibility.mjs` 0 critical/0 keyboard findings (the one `site:/` serious finding is the same pre-existing one), `check-performance.mjs` all four targets within budget. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1255 nodes / 1509 edges. No migration, no new env var, no network beyond 127.0.0.1 and npm. Did not push; on branch `ws-r63-room-dialogs-in-view`.

**2026-09-05, WS-R66 built (the creator's public page at /c/<slug>, migration 115), on worktree branch `ws-r66-creators-public-page` over a414c7c, not merged, not pushed.** WS-R45 lists a creator on `/creators` (name, one-line bio, language) and WS-R40 gives `/r/<slug>` a `<head>` a crawler can unfurl (a title and one sentence), but neither gave a search engine or a first-time visitor anything to actually READ. This is that page. Migration 115: `vy_room_showcase(id, room_id, question, answer, position, created_at, removed_at)`, real FK CASCADE from `vy_room`, a partial unique index on `(room_id, position) where removed_at is null` that IS the database's own five-slot ceiling (position CHECK-bounded 1..5, so the index makes six active rows structurally impossible rather than merely discouraged). No person column; deleted by name in `api/_replica-full-erasure.js`'s cascade (child of `vy_room`, keyed by the same room-id subquery `vy_room_arrival`/`vy_room_org_attachment` already use) with its own `owner_room_showcase` receipt class, since unlike those two this table holds real text a stranger could read. `api/_room-publish.js` gains `readRoomShowcase`, `setRoomShowcase`, `removeRoomShowcase`: a slot is either typed/edited text or copied from a review card, and THE COLUMN THAT TELLS A FOLLOWER'S OWN WORDS APART FROM CREATOR MATERIAL IS `kind` — migration 074's own comment names `'follower_declined'` as the one kind sourced from a real follower's turn, so the eligible-card SELECT is `kind <> 'follower_declined' and state = 'sounds_right'`, both inside the one WHERE, never checked after the row is in hand (`decisions.md#ws-r66-showcase-eligibility-is-a-where-clause-on-kind`). `api/creator-page.js` (thin) over `api/_creator-page.js` (pure): unlike `/r/<slug>`'s bot-only unfurl, EVERY request to `/c/:slug` reaches this handler — there is no client app underneath it to hand off to, this page IS the content. `publicCreatorPageRoomBySlug` restates the published+unpaused+listed predicate in one WHERE rather than composing `publicRoomBySlug` with a listed_at check applied after (`decisions.md#ws-r66-creator-page-predicate-restated-not-imported`); an unlisted Room and an unknown slug get the byte-identical platform-only page. `buildCreatorPageHtml`/`buildCreatorPageJsonLd` are pure, both locales via `?lang=`, JSON-LD is a real `Person` always plus a `FAQPage` only when a showcase exists (never an empty `mainEntity`), `og:image` reuses WS-R55's existing `/r/<slug>/og.png` rather than a new endpoint, the join control goes to `/r/<slug>?via=search` (already a valid `ROOM_ARRIVAL_VIA` value, wired since WS-R40, never exercised until now). `api/_sitemap.js` lists `/c/<slug>` beside `/r/<slug>`. Studio: `ShowcaseCard.tsx` (both locales, `src/studio/copy.ts`'s `showcase` section, WS-R52's locale mechanism) mounted in `RoomStudio.tsx`'s Share tab, typed/edited text only in v0 — the `sourceCardId` server path is fully built and tested but no screen lets an owner browse decided review cards to pick one from yet, logged with its reversal condition (`decisions.md#ws-r66-showcase-card-picker-ui-not-built-v0`). Performance and headers: `/c/:slug` joins both gates as a fifth/seventh target respectively, measured against a real fixture (`dist/creator-page-fixture.html`) generated from the SHIPPING `buildCreatorPageHtml` by a new `vite.config.ts` `closeBundle` hook rather than a hand-typed stand-in or a new named gate (`decisions.md#ws-r66-creator-page-fixture-generated-inside-the-web-build-gate`) — measured clean: LCP 448ms, CLS 0, TBT 154ms, 0KB JS, 0 CSP violations, no `unsafe-inline`/hash needed for the inline `application/ld+json` block because a compliant browser never subjects a non-JavaScript-MIME `<script>` to `script-src` at all (`measurements.md#ws-r66-creator-page-performance-2026-09-05`, `#ws-r66-security-headers-and-copy-gate-2026-09-05`). New `evals/creator-page/run.mjs` (55/55, registered in `evals/run.mjs`): the three-condition read gate, the pure builders, and the three required negative controls — (a) a `follower_declined` card refused as a showcase source even at `state='sounds_right'`, (b) a sixth slot (`position=6`) refused before any SQL runs and an occupied-slot overwrite never yields six active rows (a fake-db 23505 proves the retiring UPDATE is load-bearing), (c) an unlisted Room's page byte-identical to an unknown slug's. `evals/room-doors/run.mjs` gained `showcase_set`/`showcase_remove` owner-bearer cases and their `OP_COVERAGE` entries (503/503 total). `evals/room-leak/run.mjs` gained a new layer 7 (91/91 total): a static scan proving no follower-facing file ever calls the showcase writer, a world check with a real eligible card and a real follower-sourced card seeded with a unique token, and a negative control that strikes the eligibility predicate and proves the token then leaks — the mirror image of layers 1-6, which all prove a follower's words can never reach the creator; this proves the creator's own writer can never surface a follower's words either. One real bug caught and fixed on the way: a new erasure-cascade comment naming a sibling table by name (with or without backticks) broke, first the whole file's parse (a literal backtick inside the one big template literal), then room-leak's own line-scoped "every mention of vy_room_arrival is a delete" scan — fixed by describing the sibling tables generically instead (`rejected.md#ws-r66-comment-naming-a-table-broke-a-line-scoped-erasure-scan`). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: **18 of 21 on the untouched tree** (`layout readability` and `performance budgets` both `EADDRINUSE` from sibling worktrees sharing 8931/8932; `accessibility` failed on one pre-existing `site:/` color-contrast finding unrelated to this workstream, all three reproduced on a414c7c before any edit and are therefore environmental per this repo's own rule) and clean on every suite this workstream touched after its own changes (`creator-page` 55/55, `room-doors` 503/503, `room-leak` 91/91, `room-export` 44/44, `studio-locale` 40/40, `studio-shell` 65/65, `check-copy.mjs` 6 scopes/21 controls unchanged, `check-headers.mjs` and `check-performance.mjs` both green with `/c/:slug` added as a new target each). `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1259 nodes / 1511 edges. No new env var. Migration 115's two statements are new SQL never run against a live Postgres — every statement is listed verbatim in this workstream's final report for the main loop to `EXPLAIN`; no real `vy_room_showcase` row has ever existed outside a fake `db`. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: nobody has opened `/c/<slug>` in a real browser against a real deployment; no real creator has saved a showcase slot from the studio; the sourceCardId path has never been exercised from a UI, only from the API directly. Did not push; on branch `ws-r66-creators-public-page`.

Migration 115 (`vy_room_showcase`, five slots by a partial unique index, CHECK-bounded lengths) was applied live, read back, and its statements `EXPLAIN`ed; the erasure delete seq-scanned because the partial index ignores removed rows, so a plain room index was added to 115, the schema and live (`measurements.md#rooms-migration-115-live-verification-2026-09-05`). `/c/<slug>` is server-rendered for a listed, published, unpaused Room only, with the bio, up to five answers the creator typed or picked from a Sounds-right review card whose `kind` is not a follower's, JSON-LD Person and FAQPage, the Room's own og.png, and the join into `/r/<slug>?via=search`; unlisted and unknown are byte-identical; the sitemap lists it; the page joins the headers and performance gates as a generated fixture. Not built: a picker that lists decided review cards (`decisions.md#ws-r66-showcase-card-picker-ui-not-built-v0`). creator-page 55/55, leak battery 201/201, door battery 542/542, studio-locale 50/50.
