**2026-09-05, WS-R81 built (the push worker takes every kind, no migration), on worktree branch `ws-r81-push-worker-every-kind` over `6deaf1e`, not merged, not pushed.** `public/room-sw.js` dropped any push whose `t` was not the literal string `"checkin"` (`context/rejected.md#ws-r75-web-push-type-switch-drops-every-non-checkin-payload`), so WS-R37's renewal reminder has been silently discarded on arrival since it shipped, and WS-R75's dormancy notice never even attempted a send into that known-dead path. Rewrote the worker's push handler to a documented, closed contract, `{t, title, body, url}` (`t` in `checkin`/`renewal`/`dormancy`; an unlisted `t` is dropped and named once in a `console.warn`, never guessed at); `api/_push/webpush.js`'s `checkinPushPayload`/`renewalPushPayload` rewritten to build that shape (fixed title/body/url the worker now only displays, never composes) and a new `dormancyPushPayload` added; `api/_dormancy.js`'s `dormancySweep` now actually sends a web push where before it read the active subscriptions and discarded them (`void pushSubs`) because sending into the dead worker would only have been a second, equally invisible gap. `api/_creator-push.js`'s `creatorWeeklyPushPayload` and `api/_incidents.js`'s `incidentPushPayload` renamed `kind`/`route` to `t`/`url` to match. `public/push-sw.js` (the SIBLING worker for the creator/operator kinds, and ALSO Meera's own worker — a different product, out of this workstream's scope) reads both field-name generations as aliases and deliberately does NOT adopt the closed-kind drop, so a future Meera kind is never silently dropped by a Vyakti workstream's own list (`context/decisions.md#ws-r81-push-sw-shared-with-meera-no-closed-list`). `evals/room-push/run.mjs` gained a real-Chromium §8 (CDP `ServiceWorker.deliverPushMessage` against the real built `dist/room-sw.js`): all three kinds now show a correct notification, an unlisted kind shows nothing, and the exact pre-fix guard reproduced in a second worker at a distinct scope was dispatched the SAME renewal payload and showed NOTHING — the regression proven with the defect's own shape, not asserted in prose (`context/measurements.md#ws-r81-room-sw-push-kind-coverage-before-after-2026-09-05`). `evals/room-dormancy/run.mjs` gained a §8 proving the new send (1 push, right contract, right endpoint) and its two negative controls (unconfigured VAPID; no subscription) (`context/measurements.md#ws-r81-dormancy-web-push-now-real-2026-09-05`). Two existing static negative-control scans (`evals/room-push/run.mjs` §6, `evals/renewals/run.mjs`) banned BARE words (`title`; `body`/`message` unbounded) that the new contract legitimately needs as field names — fixed to ban the leak SHAPE instead, each with its own new negative control (`context/rejected.md#ws-r81-bare-word-bans-in-two-static-scans-would-have-flagged-the-new-contracts-own-field-names`). Gate: 19/21 clean on the first full run (`layout readability`/`performance budgets` both hit `EADDRINUSE` on 8931/8932 from concurrent sibling worktree gates — five other worktrees were running `verify-release.mjs` at the same time this session); both then run to completion standalone with no contention and pass cleanly (layout: 1989 prose blocks judged, 0 findings; performance: all 5 targets within budget, TBT 17-205ms against the 300ms budget, plus the installable-Room sub-check). Every other eval this workstream touched or extended run standalone and clean: `room-push` 69/0, `renewals` 55/0, `creator-push` 31/0, `room-dormancy` 46/0, `incidents` 39/0, `checkins` 37/0 (unmodified regression check), `room-leak` 217/0, `room-door` 564/0, `scripts/check-copy.mjs` clean. No migration, no new env var. No real Room, follower, or push subscription touched — everything proven offline or against a fixture server on `127.0.0.1`; real-browser push service interop stays unproven, unchanged from `api/_push/webpush.js`'s own long-standing note (no network route to one in this environment).

**2026-09-05, WS-R89 built (the second door battery), on worktree branch `ws-r89-second-door-battery` over `6deaf1e`, not merged, not pushed, no migration.** Wave thirteen's new doors (the taste island on the creator page, creator push subscriptions, flag dismissal, dormancy control, the poster, the self-check) had never been attacked; this workstream widens `evals/room-doors/run.mjs`'s own vocabulary with five new attack classes (§20-§24: body size, slug/id shape, cross-origin, replay/reuse, cron doors), each with its own completeness assertion computed from source, matching §0/§18's own established mechanism. **Five real findings fixed:** (1) no door in this product had a body-size cap at all — `api/_room-surface.js` gains `bodyTooLarge()` and two named ceilings (`ROOM_DOOR_BODY_CAP_BYTES` 64KiB, `ROOM_TRANSCRIPT_BODY_CAP_BYTES` 768KiB for `room.js`'s own transcript-bearing ops), wired into all 14 `req.body`-parsing doors (the other 3 webhook doors already capped their raw body at 1MB, verified not re-implemented). (2) `api/_creator-page.js`'s own slug read restated a weaker check than `api/_room-surface.js`'s `slugOf` (no ASCII-only check, no length ceiling) — now imports the shared one, which also gained NFKC normalisation (collapses compatibility duplicates like a fullwidth "ａ" to real "a"; a genuine cross-script homoglyph like Cyrillic "а" still refuses, proven both ways). (3) the taste op (public, unauthenticated, LLM-backed) had no Origin/Referer check, reachable by any third-party page embedding a fetch and spending this deployment's own money — `assertTasteOriginAllowed` fixes it; every other session-bearing op's wildcard CORS is confirmed intentional and unchanged, citing `room.js`'s own pre-existing "no cookie, no ambient credential" reasoning (`decisions.md#ws-r89-taste-gets-a-cross-origin-check-session-bearing-doors-do-not`). (4) `api/_creator-push.js`'s `subscribeCreatorPush` upserted on `(owner_user_id, endpoint)`, the pair, so a DIFFERENT owner subscribing the SAME endpoint silently created a second row rather than being refused — an application-level pre-check now refuses it (no migration; the narrow race this cannot close is named honestly). (5) Telegram's `handleOrdinaryMessage` (the conversational path, metered through `roomSay` exactly as the web door is) had no `update_id` dedup at all — a real Telegram redelivery would double-spend a follower's monthly cap and send a second reply; fixed with a reused `consume()` scope (`room_tg_update_seen`, 3h window, bounded by the retention sweep's 24h default rather than claiming permanence — `decisions.md#ws-r89-telegram-update-dedup-is-a-bounded-window-not-a-permanent-ledger`). **One real finding found and left OUT OF SCOPE, named rather than silently dropped:** `api/consolidate-sweep.js` (Meera's own memory-consolidation cron door, not Room-scoped) accepts its admin secret via a query string or request body and compares it non-constant-time — the ONE violation among fifteen cron doors this workstream read; `rejected.md#ws-r89-consolidate-sweep-secret-in-query-or-body-found-out-of-scope` has the full account, a follow-up task was attempted via `spawn_task` twice (both calls timed out after 60s without confirming queued). **Two considered, confirmed-safe non-findings, documented rather than silently assumed:** `api/_room-push.js`'s follower-endpoint reassignment on conflict stays as-is (one browser holds one Push subscription per origin, so cross-follower reassignment is a legitimate flow unlike creator push's — `decisions.md#ws-r89-follower-push-endpoint-reassignment-stays-as-is`); WhatsApp's status webhook confirmed to persist nothing a duplicate delivery could corrupt (already proven at §4, one more assertion added here). `evals/room-doors/fixtures.mjs` gained `state.roomPushSubs` and one new INSERT pattern for `vy_room_push_subscription`; `evals/creator-push/run.mjs` gained a SELECT pattern and four new cases for the same fix, applied independently there too. `node evals/room-doors/run.mjs`: 564 -> 667 (103 new cases, 0 regressions, three re-runs byte-identical). `node scripts/verify-release.mjs`: 19 of 21 on this workstream's own tree (`layout readability`/`performance budgets` both `EADDRINUSE` on 8931/8932 throughout this session — a sibling worktree's own gate holding the ports, `ws-common.md`'s own named collision, confirmed never clearing across repeated retries, touching none of this workstream's own files); every other check (including `eval suite`, `room leak battery` 217/217, `room export completeness` 46/46, `accessibility`, `security headers`, `typecheck`) passes clean. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1414 nodes / 1657 edges. No migration, no new env var, no network beyond 127.0.0.1 and npm. NOT PROVEN: no real deployment has ever received a cross-origin taste request, an oversized body, a homoglyph slug, a replayed push subscription, or a redelivered Telegram update outside this workstream's own offline evals; every proof here is against a fake `db`. Did not push; on branch `ws-r89-second-door-battery`.

**2026-09-05, WS-R84 (the Room's locale switch refetches everything server-authored), on branch `ws-r84-room-locale-switch-refetches`, not merged.** WS-R79 found and explicitly deferred a real bug: switching language mid-conversation updated every piece of client copy but never the disclosure card itself, because `roomSetLocale` returned only `{locale, session}`, never the card's own bytes, and the client had no other way to get them. Fixed at the one write that already had the fresh text in hand (`api/_room-surface.js`'s `roomSetLocale` now returns `disclosure` too, no new op), plus a second, related bug this workstream found while enumerating every server-authored string the Room renders: `TasteScreen` cached the disclosure text in its own local state rather than reading the `room` prop it already had, so a stranger who asked a taste question and then switched languages before joining also saw a stale card — fixed by deriving the render from `room.disclosure` directly. Telegram's `/hindi`/`/english` now re-send the disclosure card, in the new locale, in the same reply as the confirmation (it never did before). The account page's own disclosure was already correct by construction (verified, not merely assumed, via a new eval section). `scripts/check-accessibility.mjs` gained a new locale-switch walk that clicks the real language button against the real `room:talk` fixture (via a narrowly-scoped `fixtureLiveLocaleSwitch` prop and a `?live=1` fixture query, not a change to any other screen's behavior) and reads the real resulting DOM — proven non-vacuous by temporarily reverting the client fix and confirming it fails with a named `lang-stale-disclosure-after-switch` finding before passing again with the fix restored. `evals/room-locale/run.mjs` gained a §6 switch scenario (talk/account/taste, all three screens' post-switch disclosure checked against the real `roomDisclosureCard`, with a negative control proving the comparison is not vacuous); `evals/room-telegram/run.mjs` gained a section proving the Telegram parity, first run against the eval alone (3 of 61 new-section assertions failed, confirming the test bites) and then against the full fix (0 failed). Full gate: 21/21 without `NEON_URL` on the untouched tree (confirmed clean per-gate after two rounds of `EADDRINUSE` collisions with sibling worktrees on 8931-8933 forced re-runs of `layout readability`/`performance budgets`/`accessibility` individually) and 21/21 again with this workstream's patch applied. No migration, no new env var, no new SQL statement.

**2026-09-05, WS-R85 built (the share kit, migration 122), on worktree branch `ws-r85-share-kit` over 6deaf1e, not merged, not pushed.** One tap now gives a creator the exact text and picture for WhatsApp, an Instagram bio, a YouTube description and a Telegram channel post, each carrying its own `?via=<channel>` so the Growth line can say where followers actually came from — the funnel (WS-R40, WS-R78) had only known `share`/`embed`/`search`/`install`/`poster` until now. Migration 122 widens `vy_room_arrival_via_check` to ten values (the six migration 121 already named plus `whatsapp`/`instagram`/`youtube`/`telegram`), `api/_room-surface.js`'s `ROOM_ARRIVAL_VIA` widened in the same commit — the third restatement of WS-R59/WS-R78's "never one without the other" law (`decisions.md#ws-r85-migration-122-ships-with-the-js-allowlist-in-one-commit`). `api/_share-kit.js` (new) is a pure builder — `buildShareKit({name, slug, locale, origin, publishedAt})` returns `null` for a Room that has never published (nothing honest to share yet, `decisions.md#ws-r85-share-kit-null-before-publish`) or four rows, each with text under that platform's own real limit (WhatsApp kept to 300 of its real 65,536; Instagram 150; YouTube 5,000; Telegram 4,096 — `ShareKitError('share_kit_text_over_limit', ...)` thrown rather than truncated if a future template ever overflows, proven by a negative control), the `?via=` url, and which picture (story card for WhatsApp/Telegram, og image for YouTube, none for Instagram). Every template interpolates only `{name}`/`{url}`, never the creator's own free-text bio — Instagram's 150-character ceiling has no room for it (`decisions.md#ws-r85-share-kit-templates-carry-no-bio`, `measurements.md#ws-r85-share-kit-template-lengths-2026-09-05` measured 95-97/150 for a representative name). The four templates live canonically in `src/studio/copy.ts`/`hiCopy.ts`'s new `shareKit` section (the creator sees this exact text before copying it) and are restated byte-for-byte in `api/_share-kit.js`, `TASTE_COPY`'s own pattern crossed the other direction (`decisions.md#ws-r85-share-kit-copy-canonical-in-studio-not-room`); `evals/share-kit/run.mjs` proves the two never drift, by bundling the real `copy.ts`/`hiCopy.ts` with esbuild. `api/room-publish.js` gains one owner op, `share_kit` (POST, cased like `stats`), backed by `api/_room-publish.js`'s new `ownerRoomShareKit`. `api/_funnel.js`'s new `shareKitArrivalsThisWeek` breaks Growth down by channel, n>=5 floored per channel exactly like `shareArrivalsThisWeek`/`posterArrivalsThisWeek` — as FOUR separate aggregate-only statements, not one grouped query: a single `select via, sum(count) ... group by via` was tried first and rejected because `evals/room-leak/run.mjs`'s own `ARRIVAL_AGGREGATE_ONLY` scan refuses a bare `via` column in a select list even though this one carries no person-identifying data (`rejected.md#ws-r85-grouped-via-breakdown-query-fails-the-aggregate-only-select-list-scan`). `api/_ops.js`/`opsApi.ts`/`OpsBoard.tsx` surface the breakdown on the ops board's own Growth section. `src/studio/ShareKitCard.tsx` (new) is the Share tab's own card — four rows, a copy button (`navigator.clipboard`, with a "Copied" state and a fallback `<textarea>` select for browsers without the API), an "Open WhatsApp" deep link (`wa.me/?text=`) on the WhatsApp row, and a picture link where one exists; mounted inside `RoomStudio.tsx` (`ShowcaseCard.tsx`'s own precedent), added to `evals/studio-locale/run.mjs`'s `TIER_1_FILES` and `evals/studio-shell/run.mjs`'s orphan-check allowlist. Renders clean under `node scripts/check-layout.mjs --only studio` (both locales, the `deploy-picker`/published scenario) without needing a new fixture state — the existing `showcase-picker` scenario already publishes the fixture Room. Gates: share-kit 79/79 (new), room-share 57/57 (extended to the ten-value allowlist, cross-checked against migration 122's own CHECK text), room-doors 568/568 (the new `share_kit` op added to `room-publish.js`'s `e-owner-bearer` class and `OP_COVERAGE`), room-leak 217/217 (unaffected — the four new statements pass `ARRIVAL_AGGREGATE_ONLY`), room-export unaffected (46/46), ops 137/137 (the honest-empty-state shape extended to four channels), studio-locale 58/58, studio-shell 68/68, `check-copy.mjs`/`check-mirrors.mjs` clean, `tsc -b` clean. No new env var. What needs the live DB: migration 122's own `EXPLAIN`, and confirming `vy_room_arrival_via_check` is still the live constraint name at the moment of application (read back from `db/schema.sql`'s own record rather than re-derived, migration 121's precedent). What needs a human: nothing blocking — the templates are a first draft a human should read before this ships to real WhatsApp groups.

**2026-09-05, WS-R90 built (the creator's page in search), on worktree branch `ws-r90-creators-page-in-search` over `6deaf1e`, not merged, not pushed, no migration.** `/c/<slug>` (WS-R66) already had a canonical, Person/FAQPage JSON-LD, and per-node language tags (WS-R79); this workstream adds the three things a bilingual search result still needed: `HREFLANG_CODES`/`HI_LANG_QUERY`/`OG_LOCALE` named constants in `api/_creator-page.js` drive three `<link rel="alternate" hreflang>` tags (en, hi via `?lang=hi`, x-default) and one `<meta property="og:locale">` tag, identical on every response — including the platform-only unknown/unlisted fallback — per Google's own "the set of links is identical for every version" rule (fetched and cited, `decisions.md#ws-r90-hreflang-og-locale-identical-on-every-response`); `api/_sitemap.js`'s `urlset` gains the `xmlns:xhtml` namespace and each `/c/<slug>` entry gains the matching `xhtml:link` cluster, `/r/<slug>` deliberately left alone (`decisions.md#ws-r90-sitemap-hreflang-only-on-c-slug-not-r-slug`). `scripts/probeLiveExpectations.mjs` gains `creatorPageHeadFacts()` (parsed from `api/_creator-page.js`'s own new constants, never retyped) and two small schema.org required-field validators, `validatePersonJsonLd`/`validateFaqPageJsonLd`, shared by the eval and the live probe. `scripts/probe-live.mjs` gains an optional `--creator-slug <slug>` flag checking `/c/<slug>`'s canonical/hreflang/JSON-LD against a REAL listed Room; omitted (the honest default, since **no real `vy_room` row has ever been inserted anywhere outside a fake `db`**, this file's own LIVE table, still true), the section is skipped with a printed note, never a failure (`decisions.md#ws-r90-creator-slug-probe-flag-optional-honest-skip`). `evals/probe-live/fakeServer.mjs` gains a `/c/<slug>` route serving the REAL `buildCreatorPageHtml`'s output for one named fixture slug, plus two new defects for negative controls — its first draft forgot to call `applyHeaders`, silently dropping the CSP/HSTS headers the OLD 404 fallback had been supplying by accident, caught by the pre-existing "clean fixture, zero findings" assertion and fixed (`rejected.md#ws-r90-fake-server-c-slug-handler-forgot-applyheaders`). `evals/creator-page/run.mjs` grew from 89 to 119 assertions (hreflang/og:locale on both locales and the fallback, the JSON-LD validators against real output plus five negative controls, and the sitemap's new hreflang cluster); `evals/creator-directory/run.mjs` from 55 to 61; `evals/probe-live/run.mjs` from 12 to 23 checks. Performance: `/c/<slug>` LCP 268ms / TBT 0ms / JS 2.2KB unchanged, all within budget; the fixture HTML itself grew 7186 to 7485 bytes (+299B) for the four new head tags (`measurements.md#ws-r90-creator-page-performance-2026-09-05`, `#ws-r90-creator-page-bytes-2026-09-05`). Full gate: 21/21 without `NEON_URL` (the only failures across several runs were `EADDRINUSE` on 8931/8932/8933 from sibling worktrees' own concurrent gates, confirmed by `fuser`/`/proc/net/tcp` showing a live listener each time, reproduced on the untouched tree too, and gone on rerun). Did not push; on branch `ws-r90-creators-page-in-search`.

**2026-09-05, WS-R86 built (follower referrals, migration 123), on worktree branch `ws-r86-follower-referrals` over `6deaf1e`, not merged, not pushed.** A follower can bring a friend with a personal link; the creator learns only that referrals happen, in counts under the n>=5 floor; the follower is never named to anyone, including someone holding the table directly. Migration 123: `vy_room_referral(referral_id, room_id, referrer_hash, created_at)`, a room-aggregate table with NO person column at all — `referrer_hash` is a salted sha256 of (this Room, the referring follower's own person id), `api/_rate-limit.js`'s own `RATE_SALT` salt shape restated WITHOUT that function's daily rotation (`decisions.md#ws-r86-referral-hash-reuses-rate-salt-without-daily-rotation`) — a link has to keep comparing equal to itself for as long as it is shared. `vy_room_arrival.via` widened to admit `'friend'` alongside the six values migration 121 named, in the same commit. `api/_room-surface.js` gains `referralHashFor` (the one hash function, three callers: minting, self-referral comparison, and a follower's own recomputed export count), `roomReferralLink` (session-scoped, `selfScope`'s own gate, returns a RELATIVE `/r/<slug>?via=friend&ref=<hash>` path, never absolute), and `joinRoom` gains a `ref` parameter: the referral write is an INSERT-SELECT whose own WHERE clause (`referrer_hash <> joiner_hash`) is the entire self-referral guard (`decisions.md#ws-r86-self-referral-refused-in-the-insert-where-not-a-js-if`), gated to a follower's genuinely FIRST-EVER join via the canonical Postgres `(xmax = 0) as newly_joined` idiom read off the SAME upsert that creates or updates the follower row (`decisions.md#ws-r86-referral-credited-on-xmax-new-row-not-a-session-flag`) — a repeat join (the memory toggle) with the same `ref` still in a follower's browser mints no second row. `roomExport` gains one special-cased read (not one of the eleven `ROOM_EXPORT_EXTRA` entries, since the table carries no `person_id` for that loop's generic shapes): a follower's own referral count, computed by recomputing their OWN hash — nobody but their own export process can ever perform that recomputation. New op `referral_link` on `api/room.js`, rate-limited under a new `room_referral_follower` scope (10/day, `api/_rate-limit.js`), cased in the door battery (two new class-a/b cases, `join`'s own exclusion reasoning extended to name `ref`). `api/_funnel.js` gains two reads: `friendArrivalsThisWeek` (platform-wide, `via='friend'` over the existing `vy_room_arrival`, wired into the ops board's Growth line) and `friendsBroughtThisWeek` (per-room, over `vy_room_referral`, wired into `api/_room-publish.js`'s `ownerRoomStats` for the Room Studio's own "Friends brought this week" card) — both n>=5 floored. `api/_replica-full-erasure.js` deletes the table by room, child before parent, no new receipt class (content-free, `vy_room_arrival`'s own precedent). `api/_creator-export.js` names it a deliberate gap (excluded from the owner-lane export by construction — no owning column, no person column — documented rather than left silent). Front end: `src/room/AccountPage.tsx` gets a "Bring a friend" card under the disclosure (both locales, `src/room/copy.ts`'s new `referral` section), fed by a `fixtureReferralUrl` seam threaded through `RoomApp.tsx` so the layout gate actually renders it; `src/studio/RoomStudio.tsx` and the ops board (`OpsBoard.tsx`/`opsApi.ts`) render the two funnel counts. **A real bug found and fixed by the layout gate itself**: the URL display first reused `.room-num` (a numeral font-variant, unrelated to wrapping) and overflowed 156px sideways on a 390px phone with 193px of its own text clipped; fixed with a new `.room-referral-url { overflow-wrap: anywhere }` class, `.room-bubble`'s own precedent, reconfirmed clean afterward (`rejected.md#ws-r86-referral-url-display-reused-room-num-a-numeric-class-not-a-wrap-one`). **evals/room-leak/run.mjs gains layer 13** (world isolation across two independent Rooms, a negative control proving the room-scoped WHERE is load-bearing by summing across Rooms when struck, and a negative control proving a reader selecting `referrer_hash` itself — as opposed to only counting — is caught by the same generalized TABLE_ROLES mechanism `vy_room_arrival` also uses). **New suite `evals/room-referrals/run.mjs`** (41/41): the hash's own properties, the link's exact shape, credit-once-on-first-join, self-referral refused (plus a genuine negative control: a struck copy of the write DOES leak a self-referral row), five malformed-ref shapes refused before reaching SQL, an uppercase real hash still credited (case-insensitive), the two funnel reads' floor behavior, and the export law. Two real, non-obvious bugs caught mid-session and logged: a fifth occurrence of the SQL-comment-backtick trap (`rejected.md#ws-r86-sql-comment-backticks-terminate-the-template-literal-a-fifth-time`), and a second occurrence of a table name mentioned in a comment tripping the bespoke `vy_room_arrival` scanner in `api/_creator-export.js` (`rejected.md#ws-r86-creator-export-comment-naming-vy-room-arrival-tripped-its-bespoke-scanner`) — both existing, named traps in this repo's own history, repeated a session later regardless of having been read beforehand, caught by `node --check` and the room-leak battery respectively rather than by having remembered the rule. A third: the room-share suite's own `ROOM_ARRIVAL_VIA`/CHECK cross-check had hardcoded migration 121's own filename and broke the moment migration 123 widened the same constraint again — rewritten to read `db/schema.sql`'s own LAST matching block instead, so a future workstream widening this constraint a further time cannot make it stale again (`decisions.md#ws-r86-room-share-via-check-cross-check-reads-schema-not-a-migration-file`). Gate results: baseline (untouched tree, separate worktree at `6deaf1e`) 18/21, the three failures all reproduced as the SAME named environmental collisions (`EADDRINUSE` 8931/8933, `performance budgets` one TBT finding under load) this repo's own session log already documents repeatedly. This workstream's own changed tree: **19/21 in one full `verify-release.mjs` run** — `layout readability`/`performance budgets` both `EADDRINUSE` mid-run (sibling worktrees, confirmed by PID/cwd), every other gate clean including `accessibility` (0 findings) and `security headers`; `layout readability` was THEN confirmed standalone (`--only room`, after the CSS fix above): clean, 221 prose blocks, 225 Hindi strings glyph-checked, 0 findings. `node evals/run.mjs`: exit 0. `npx tsc -b`: clean. `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: clean, 6 scopes, 21 negative controls, unchanged. `node scripts/context.mjs --check`: clean, 1415 nodes / 1657 edges. `door battery` 564 -> 568; `leak battery` 217 -> 229, twelve layers -> thirteen; `room-export` 46 -> 47; `ops` 135 -> 136; `room-share` 54 -> 56. **New SQL for the main loop's own EXPLAIN**: migration 123's `create table`/`create index`/two `alter table` statements; `joinRoom`'s widened RETURNING clause and its new conditional `insert into vy_room_referral ... select ... where`; `roomExport`'s new `select count(*)::int as n from vy_room_referral where room_id = $1 and referrer_hash = $2`; `friendsBroughtThisWeek`'s `select count(*)::int as n from vy_room_referral where room_id = $1 and created_at >= $2`; `friendArrivalsThisWeek`'s `select coalesce(sum(count),0)::int as n from vy_room_arrival where via = 'friend' and day >= $1`; the erasure cascade's new `room_referrals` CTE. No new env var. NOT PROVEN, stated plainly: no statement here has ever run against a live Postgres (no `NEON_URL` in this environment); no real `vy_room_referral` row exists anywhere outside a fake `db`; `scripts/relcheck.mjs` did not run. Coordination note for the merge: migration 123's CHECK constraint on `vy_room_arrival.via` is written against this tree's own six pre-existing values plus `friend`; WS-R85 (migration 122, the share kit, running concurrently) may add its own value(s) to the SAME constraint — the main loop reconciles the union at the merge, per this workstream's own brief. Did not push; on branch `ws-r86-follower-referrals`.

**2026-09-05, WS-R83 (wave fourteen).** The six consent-ceremony studio files WS-R61 and WS-R71 held back from Hindi conversion (`ModelConsentGate.tsx`, `IdentityProofing.tsx`, `VideoEnrollPanel.tsx`, `IngestChannelStudio.tsx`, `LivenessCapture.tsx`, `VoiceIdentityChallenge.tsx`) now have proposed Hindi in `docs/legal/HINDI-CONSENT-REVIEW.md`, a standalone legal-review document: 88 rows (26 consent statements, every ceremony heading/legend/primary action/boundary line, and File 6's own `REASON` map standing in for its missing checkbox array), each with the English exactly as shipped, a proposed Hindi, and a plain-English back-translation, plus the `statement_set`/`policy_version` id each row is recorded against (`decisions.md#ws-r83-consent-ceremony-hindi-review-document-before-conversion`). No `src/` change. `evals/consent-review/run.mjs` (registered in `evals/run.mjs`, a release-gate suite) re-extracts every row's source text from the real six files on every run and proves the document complete against them, runs the real `scripts/check-copy.mjs` scanner over all 88 proposed Hindi rows (0 offences, three negative controls firing correctly), and cross-checks the cited statement_set/policy_version ids against the real exported constants: 116/116 assertions pass (`measurements.md#ws-r83-hindi-consent-review-coverage-2026-09-05`). Three rows (two in `ModelConsentGate.tsx`, one in `LivenessCapture.tsx`) render a Rooms-vocabulary-banned English word ("training", "model", "embeddings", "replica") functionally in Hindi rather than transliterating it, and are flagged in the document as needing explicit legal confirmation the paraphrase does not narrow consent scope. The untouched-tree baseline gate ran 21/21 (a first attempt hit two `EADDRINUSE` collisions on 8931/8933 from a sibling worktree's gate and an unrelated `sound` suite flake that a clean rerun did not reproduce); the gate with this workstream's changes also ran 21/21, `eval suite` included. Nothing converts yet: this document is input to legal sign-off, not the sign-off itself.

**2026-09-05, WS-R82 built (the studio's last four files in Hindi, and a Hindi first-paint budget), on worktree branch `ws-r82-studio-hindi-last-four-first-paint` over `6deaf1e`, not merged, not pushed.** Three of the four named files convert: `ContextLockerPanel.tsx`, `MirrorCallStudio.tsx`, `VoiceEnrollmentLab.tsx` (264 new leaf strings per locale — 78 + 120 + 66 — `copy.ts`/`hiCopy.ts` gain their own closed sections at the end, whole-table total now 1,452 real Hindi strings). `layoutFixture.tsx` and `main.tsx` reclassify from `TIER_2_ALLOWLIST` into `TIER_1_FILES` (zero literal text of their own, the `localeContext.tsx`/`Localized.tsx` precedent, not a translation). The fourth named file, `EnrollmentWorkspace.tsx`, is deliberately NOT converted: read in full, its consent-panel article carries a live, four-statement `attestations` array — the same formal consent-ceremony shape as `ModelConsentGate.tsx`'s six `STATEMENTS`, making it a SEVENTH file of that shape, found only after this wave's own sibling WS-R83 had already fixed its own legal-review scope at exactly the six WS-R61/WS-R71 found (`scratchpad/ws-r83-consent-ceremonies-hindi-review.md`, visible from this session's own scratchpad). A full extraction (`EnrollmentConsentPanel.tsx`, the same shape `PayoutsCard.tsx` etc. were carved out of `RoomStudio.tsx`) was built and type-checked clean, then deliberately reverted rather than shipped — see `context/rejected.md#ws-r82-enrollment-consent-panel-extracted-then-reverted`. `ContextLockerPanel.tsx`'s own one consent-shaped checkbox (flagged by WS-R71 for a closer read) was read closely and found to be a feature-gating control, not a ceremony, and converted with the rest of the file (`decisions.md#ws-r82-context-locker-checkbox-is-a-control-not-a-ceremony`). Two real, previously-invisible defects were caught by the move into `copy.ts` (a whole-file scan `isVisibleLiteral()` a bare JSX ternary or function-return literal never triggered before): `MirrorCallStudio.tsx`'s "A voice fine-tune is queued" (banned vocabulary, reworded to "voice build," this product's own established substitution) and `ContextLockerPanel.tsx`'s "...applied to your clone..." (reworded to "...your AI..."); a THIRD, independent defect (not a copy-gate finding) was also caught by reading rather than testing: the review tab hardcoded "8-per-minute" as English prose describing the chip rail's cap, but the real `CHIPS_PER_MINUTE` constant is 3 — fixed by interpolating the real constant into both templates rather than shipping a wrong number newly visible in two languages. `scripts/check-performance.mjs` gains a `studio-hi` target (`/studio?lang=hi`, same LCP/JS budgets as `/studio`) and a named `hindiChunkWaitMs` metric — NOT a literal "first Hindi text node" read, because `StudioApp.tsx`'s `AuthGate` renders before `StudioLocaleProvider` mounts and the signed-out entry paints zero Hindi regardless of `?lang=hi` (`rejected.md#ws-r82-studio-hi-signed-out-entry-never-shows-hindi`) — instead a real `import()` of the actual built `dist/assets/hiCopy-*.js` chunk, timed from the page's own `first-paint` entry under the gate's throttle, with the chunk's own bytes excluded from the JS budget tally (a real signed-out visitor never requests it; counting it would fail the JS budget on a request only this gate's own measurement causes). **Measured for the first time**, WS-R71's own open reversal condition: median 583-636ms across repeated n=3 runs against an 800ms budget — passes with real margin, no `<link rel="modulepreload">` needed this session. `node evals/studio-locale/run.mjs`: 62/62. `node scripts/check-copy.mjs`: 6 scopes clean, 21 negative controls. `npx tsc -b --noEmit`: clean. Gate re-run at the end: 21/21 without `NEON_URL` (see the session's own final report for the exact summary line). No migration; no new env var. Did not push; on branch `ws-r82-studio-hindi-last-four-first-paint`.
